- **v350:** **Automated test suite — slice 1 (build spec from `HEARTH-tests-review.md`, PR #110, per the design-here/build-on-Sonnet handoff; D1–D5 confirmed by Cathal 25/07/2026, D3/D6 defaulted to the review's recommendation).** New `tests/` directory: a **zero-dependency CDP harness** (`tests/harness.js`, ~140 lines, Node ≥22 built-in `fetch`/`WebSocket` only — no Playwright, no npm, no `node_modules`, no lockfile) spawns the pre-installed headless Chromium with a `--host-resolver-rules` network block and a fresh `--user-data-dir` per run, connects over raw CDP, and exposes `navigate`/`evaluate`/`reset`/`pageErrors`/`close`. `tests/run.js` boots `index.html` from `file://`, runs each of 8 case files in `tests/cases/` (fresh `localStorage.clear()` + re-navigate between files for isolation), asserts zero page errors throughout, and prints `::error::` annotations on failure so a red PR shows what broke in plain English on the Checks tab. **Cases:** `01-boot-smoke` (all 9 sections + every visible bottom-nav button, real DOM clicks), `02-merge-lists`/`03-merge-hist`/`04-merge-baby`/`05-merge-trips` (pure merge-function unit tests — union, newest-wins, tombstones, the v296 generic-key carry-through, the v348 hist cap), `06-trip-roundtrip` (the 11-field export/import round-trip + error paths), `07-backup-roundtrip` (seed-through-real-functions → export → wipe → import, incl. the v323 hist-tombstone-survives-import case), `08-dose-e2e` (real `.click()` on the Calpol/Nurofen chips at 6 weight scenarios incl. the round-down-to-0.25 and cap cases — **no dosing code touched**, D5-confirmed). **D2 accepted:** one-line nameless-entry guard in both `mergeHist` and `capHistToLiveLimit` (`h.name` now checked before `.toLowerCase()`) — a hand-corrupted backup import no longer throws; verified by temporarily reverting the guard and confirming `03-merge-hist` catches the regression (both the throw and the correct behaviour are now pinned). **D4 accepted:** `HEARTH-notes.md`'s Recipe unit rules corrected — ingredient counts scale via `fmtNumber`, not to whole numbers (e.g. "1 ½ cloves" is correct, matches code). **CI wiring** (`.github/workflows/hearth-pr-checks.yml`): a new "Behaviour tests" step runs `node tests/run.js` (no install step); the existing "Verify" step gained dosing literal-pin greps (Calpol/Nurofen `data-mgkg`/`data-mg5`/`data-floor`, the formula string, "check the leaflet" count) and a duplicate-top-level-function-name grep. **Unplanned finding from the duplicate-function grep:** ~24 function names (a whole workout-session-shaped region — `beginWorkout`, `t2`, `startYogaTimer`, `saveSession`, etc.) are each defined **twice** — not the `openSetSheet`/`closeSetSheet` pair the review anticipated (that pair is NOT duplicated). One copy is silently dead via JS hoisting. **Cathal's call (25/07/2026): allowlist the current 24 names for now (CI only fails on NEW duplicates going forward), investigate which copy is live/dead as a separate backlog item** (see Pending section). D3 (branch protection) defaulted to report-only per the review's recommendation, not yet asked explicitly. D6 (accept the sw.js + Firebase-transport test gaps) defaulted to accepted, matching the review's recommendation. No production data-model, export, or sync-protocol changes beyond the D2 guard.
- **v349:** **Chart redesign — one shared visual language for the three trend charts (growth centile, weight history, BP history; Cathal request 22/07/2026 "improve the look", designed+built on Fable).** New top-level shared helpers (top-level on purpose — renderer-called): `chartDots` (white-ringed dots r3, emphasized r4.5 latest point — readable where dots cross lines/bands), `chartEndLabel` (bold ink value label at the line end, baseline-clamped inside the plot), `chartDateLabels` (first/last date, warm-gray), `chartTicks` (2–4 round-number gridline values), `centileOrdinal`. All three SVG builders redrawn to one spec: height 126→140, 2px round-capped/joined lines, axis text `#8A8578` (was low-contrast `#B8A8B8`), and the old padded min/max y-labels replaced by labels at **meaningful** values — growth labels 15/50/85 (band edges + median), BP labels its own thresholds 80/90/130/140, weight gets round-number hairline gridlines (`#EFE9DC`) via `chartTicks`. Per-chart: growth keeps the 15th–85th band (softened `#EBF5E4`) + dashed median (`#C4BCAE`), latest point end-labelled as an ordinal ("62nd"); weight adds an 8%-opacity area wash under the line and a "NN.N kg" end label (padR widened to 46 so the label never clips); BP bands softened (`#FCF0EC`/`#FDF6E6`/`#EDF6E7`), sys+dia latest values end-labelled, and the cramped one-line caption replaced by a colour-key legend row (solid/dashed line swatches, "Systolic"/"Diastolic") + a shorter muted bands caption. Series colours unchanged and validated (sys/dia pair passes colourblind-separation + contrast checks; the dia dash pattern doubles as the non-colour channel). Growth-card caption updated to match ("dashed line = WHO median"). Render-only — no data-model, export, or sync changes; all three charts screenshot-verified in headless Chromium with sample data before commit. (The v340 label-collision fix is superseded by this layout — its min/max labels no longer exist.)
- **v348:** **Grocery autocomplete/suggestion hist/items divergence fix — both parts (design confirmed on Fable 21/07/2026 via PR #107, "blueberries" incident; built build-ready per the 12/07 handoff policy).** Root cause: `showAC` (while-typing autocomplete) and `buildGrocerySuggestions` (Today-card suggestions) both read ONLY the list's `hist` array, so a name that's still visibly in the list (active or Done) gets no autocomplete/chip once its hist entry dies — via the 150-entry cap prune, a v323 history deletion tombstone, or a backup import. **Part 1 — `showAC` items fallback:** when hist matches are fewer than 7, now scans the current list's `getItems()` for substring matches not already found in hist, fills the remaining slots (hist matches first, item matches after, 7 total, exact-match still excluded). Item-derived rows reuse the existing `.ac-item` layout with the category badge, but show a new `.ac-tag` state span ("on list" / "done") instead of the "N×" count. Tap/Add flow unchanged. Deliberately ignores hist tombstones — a name still visibly on the list suggests again (Cathal's stated expectation), matching a live entry's existing behaviour. Device-local read logic only, zero sync changes. **Part 2 — cap protection at source:** new top-level `capHistToLiveLimit(sortedHist, items)` exempts any hist entry whose name matches a current list item (active or Done) from the 150-cap; only unprotected entries are capped, so hist can now exceed 150 by at most the list's item count (accepted, lists are small). Wired into all 4 prune sites: `mergeHist` (gained a 4th `items` param, called from all three sync/import sites — `importBackupData`, the personal-sync channel, the household-sync channel — each already had the merged items array in scope), `addToHist`, and `addToHistFor` (the latter previously didn't sort before capping at all — now sorts first like `addToHist`, so the cap logic can meaningfully keep the highest-value unprotected entries). A v323 deletion tombstone still beats cap protection (explicit user act; unaffected). Mixed-version safe — an older partner device still hard-caps at 150, but the existing-name-wins union in `mergeHist` restores the entry from the local copy on the next merge. `buildGrocerySuggestions` itself needed no direct change — cap protection keeps the hist entries it reads alive at the source. No data-model, export, or protocol changes.
- **v347:** **Baby growth chart — true WHO weight-for-length centiles (Tier-2 backlog item, design confirmed 19/07/2026, built 21/07/2026, PR #106).** Replaces the v310 "% of WHO median" ratio chart with a genuine LMS-method centile chart, same as real WHO/CDC growth charts. New `WHO_WFL_LMS` (L/M/S parameters, boys+girls, 45–110cm at 5cm steps, linearly interpolated — same tradeoff the old medians-only table already made), `whoWflLMS()` interpolator, `normalCdf()` (Abramowitz–Stegun standard-normal-CDF approximation, no library), `growthCentile()` (z = ((weight/M)^L − 1)/(L·S), centile = Φ(z)×100), and `growthCentileChartSvg()` (replaces `growthRatioChartSvg`, which is fully removed — no dead second copy). `growthBand()` and `growthTrendSummary()` now report real centiles (5 tiers: <3rd/3rd–15th/15th–85th/85th–97th/>97th; badge text like "≈ 62nd centile") instead of the old Below-avg/Normal/Above-avg ratio labels — calm, descriptive tone kept, never advice/alarm language. **Data-sourcing snag, handled per the hard rule in this file's own build spec ("any disagreement means STOP and investigate, not proceed"):** `who.int` and `cdc.gov` are both blocked by this session's network policy (confirmed at the proxy level, not a site error), so the LMS tables were pulled from a GitHub mirror of the same CDC-redistributed WHO CSVs instead (identical `Length,L,M,S,percentiles` column structure) and cross-checked — L is genuinely constant per sex across all 131 half-cm rows in the source (a real WHO quirk for this indicator, not a transcription error). The new M (median) values disagreed with the app's old "approximate" `WHO_WFL` table by up to ~3.5% at longer lengths; **stopped mid-build and asked Cathal via AskUserQuestion, who confirmed proceeding with the newly-sourced data** (full comparison table in the PR body). No data-model, export, or sync changes — render-only; Family Log and every other growth-entry consumer unaffected (verified no other call sites reference the removed `growthMedianForLength`/`GROWTH_BAND_LO`/`GROWTH_BAND_HI`/`growthRatioChartSvg`). Manual on-device look at the chart itself is still outstanding — flagged for Cathal in the PR alongside the "spot-check a value against the HSE book" ask.
- **v346:** **Medicine "bottle opened / freshness" tracking (designed on Opus 20/07/2026, all decisions confirmed by Cathal; built same day per the design-here-build-on-Sonnet handoff).** New **product/inventory** layer on top of the dose log — lets Cathal record when a Calpol/Nurofen/other bottle was opened so the app can flag when it's likely no longer good, independently of the printed expiry. **Does NOT touch dosing:** `MED_RULES`, the dosing chips, weight-based formulas, strengths, and the v291 interval advisories are all untouched; this feature only reads product identity from `MED_RULES`' keys. **The "use within N days" window is a reminder the USER sets** — never presented as a manufacturer/leaflet fact; prefilled with a soft, editable 90-day suggestion, always paired with "check the leaflet". **Data model:** new `bd.bottles` map keyed by product key (`"calpol"`/`"nurofen"` — deliberately the same keys as `MED_RULES` so the dose-log advisory can cross-reference; free-form bottles get a generated `"b"+Date.now()` key), each entry `{key, name, openedTs, useWithinDays, updated}`. New top-level `bottleFreshness(b)` computes `discardTs = openedTs + useWithinDays*86400000` → status `"past"` (red, now≥discardTs) / `"due"` (amber, within 14d of discard) / `"fresh"` (green). **Surfacing, all three per spec:** **(a)** a "Bottle freshness" card at the very top of `renderMedicine()` (above the log card) — Calpol/Nurofen rows always shown (with "not tracking" + Mark opened when absent), plus any free-form bottles; each tracked row shows opened/window/discard dates + status dot, with Edit (inline date+window picker, mirrors the existing `.med-dt-edit` pattern) / New bottle (confirm-gated, resets `openedTs` to now) / remove controls, and a "+ Add another bottle" button (custom name+window overlay — `prompt()` is blocked in sandboxed iframes, so it follows the existing "Name this meal" overlay pattern); **(b)** the `medLogBtn` dose-log advisory now also cross-checks `bd.bottles[rule.key]` and folds a bottle-past-window line into the same OK/Cancel speed-bump as the v291 interval advisory (combines with it when both fire) — advisory only, never blocks; **(c)** a compact amber note on the Home Today baby card (new `#tcBabyBottleNote` div, hidden unless a tracked bottle is actually past its window). **Sync:** `getBD()` default-inits `bd.bottles={}`; the household push guard's non-empty check now includes `Object.keys(bd.bottles||{}).length`; new top-level `mergeBottles(a,b)` (per-key newest-wins by `updated`, tie→remote) is called from both `mergeBabyData` (new `bottles` key in `merged`) and `importBackupData`'s baby block. **No tombstones in v1** (deliberate — "New bottle" reset is the common op, not delete; documented low-stakes resurrect risk, same class as `HEARTH-notes.md`'s other no-tombstone calls). **Box/printed expiry NOT tracked in v1** (after-opening window only). **Flagged for Cathal in the PR:** (1) confirm the real after-opening windows from his actual bottles and set them (the 90-day prefill is a placeholder, not medical guidance), (2) OK the baby-facing wording per the baby-wording rule. **Same-PR fix (Cathal's on-device catch, 20/07/2026):** the initial build shipped both "Opened today" and "New bottle" doing the identical `openedTs=now` reset — v1 keeps no bottle history, so "corrected the date" and "swapped in a fresh bottle" are indistinguishable state-wise. Removed "Opened today"; "New bottle" (confirm-gated) is now the only reset control.
- **v345:** **Back button v2 — generic close for the 8 one-off dynamic dialogs + trip-editor back-as-cancel (design confirmed on Fable 19/07/2026, build-ready per the 12/07 handoff policy; built 20/07/2026).** v330 shipped the close-order core (`closeTopOverlay`) but deliberately excluded the dynamically-created `role="dialog"`/`role="alertdialog"` sheets and the trip/booking editors. **(1) Generic dynamic-dialog step:** each of the 8 sheets' existing safe-dismiss button (cloud backups `#cbClose`, sync-health `#shClose`, meal-plan picker `#mpPickClose`, meal-plan day sheet `#mpCancelBtn`, sports manage `#sptClose`, voice review `#vcCancel`, delete-all-data confirm `#_delCancelBtn`, recipe-clear 3-way `#_rcCancel`) got a one-line `data-bbclose` tag; a new second step in `closeTopOverlay()` (placed right after the `#_cfNo` confirmDialog check, before the static-sheet checks) finds the topmost `body > div[role="dialog"], body > div[role="alertdialog"]` and clicks its `[data-bbclose]` button — back-press runs the exact same code path as tapping the sheet's own Cancel/Close, never a reimplemented close; an untagged future sheet falls through without closing anything. **(2) Trip/booking editor discard-confirm:** new module global `_tripCancelFn` (beside `_tripEditing`), pointed at each editor's own cancel closure on open (`openTripEditor`/`openBookingEditor`) and nulled on every exit path (cancel/save/delete, 6 sites) plus `renderTrips()`'s existing entry-reset. A new LAST check in `closeTopOverlay()` (after all dialog checks, since a confirmDialog can still be stacked above the editor): if `_tripEditing && _tripCancelFn`, asks "Discard changes?" via the existing `confirmDialog()` — OK runs the cancel closure, Cancel leaves the editor open; a second back-press while that confirm is up hits step 1 and cancels the confirm instead, consistent with the rest of the mechanism. **Stale-flag side-fix from the design turned out already fixed:** `renderTrips()` already reset `_tripEditing = false` on every section entry (present since v312) — the backlog's "nothing resets it on navigate-away" concern didn't apply; only `_tripCancelFn` needed adding to that same reset line, to avoid a stale closure over removed DOM. Pure UI/navigation change — no data-model, export, or sync changes.
- **v344:** **Add "panadol" to the paracetamol advisory match list (confirmed item from the 19/07/2026 Fable design day — Cathal photographed the household medicine bottles; adults use Panadol Extra Soluble).** One word added to the calpol rule's `match` array in `MED_RULES` (~line 11450): `["calpol","paracetamol"]` → `["calpol","paracetamol","panadol"]`. Advisory tracking only — a hand-typed "Panadol" dose now counts toward the paracetamol interval advisory (≥4h gap, max 4/24h); no dose chip, no strength/formula/interval changes, no dosing logic touched. No data-model, export, or sync changes.
- **v343:** **Notes sync hardening + work notes sync join (sync audit fix list items ①② from the Fable audit 19/07/2026, PR #102; the highest-priority open item — active, user-visible notes-loss risk, not hypothetical).** **①** Both notes stores previously synced worse than every other data type: the household channel wholesale-replaced personal notes with whatever the last-pushing device sent, and the personal channel's per-id union had no recency check (an older backend copy could overwrite a newer unpushed edit). Both replaced by a new shared `mergeNotes(local, incoming, tombs)` helper (id-keyed union, newest-wins by `updatedAt||id` — pre-existing notes merge correctly with no migration — tombstone-filtered), mirroring the `mergeTripsData`/`mergeBabyData` shape. New tombstone namespaces `fl4_tomb_notes_p`/`fl4_tomb_notes_w`, written on delete, synced as `notes_deleted`/`worknotes_deleted` on both channels (mirrors `med_deleted`'s placement) — note deletions now actually propagate instead of only "propagating" via the old wholesale-replace. **②** Work notes (`fl4_notes_global_work`) were never included in either push payload or apply path — stranded per-device (backed up, never synced). Now joins both channels under the existing `isSynced("notes")` gate, same as Personal notes. **Implementation:** `NOTES_CONTEXTS` gained `tomb`/`pfield`/`dfield` properties so `pushPersonal`/`applyPersonal`/`pushHousehold`/`applyHousehold` all drive both contexts off one loop instead of four hand-duplicated personal/work code paths — can't drift out of sync with each other again. No data-model or export changes; `fl4_tomb_*` stays un-exported per policy. Item ③ (workouts-rebuild generic key carry-through + duplicate `lastPushTs`) remains open, gated on Train/Track next being touched.
- **v342:** **Fix suggestion chips reappearing right after a matching item is ticked off (Cathal screenshot report 19/07/2026, "Bananas"; root-caused on Fable same day).** Two holes in the v306 suggestion logic: **(a)** `buildGrocerySuggestions` suppressed a chip only while a matching item was ACTIVE (`!it.done`) — ticking the item off removed the suppression, and nothing counted the purchase as a signal, so an item whose history was already "due" (e.g. often-bought, last add >10 days ago) re-suggested instantly; **(b)** the revive branch of `addItemToCurrent` (an add matching a Done item — exactly what tapping a suggestion chip does for a staple sitting in Done) never called `addToHist`, so `lastUsed`/`dates`/`count` stayed stale for precisely the most re-bought items, also degrading the v303 replenishment data. **Fix:** (1) the suggestion builder now collects a `doneTs` map (each done item's `updated` stamp, which `toggleItem` sets on every tick) and uses `max(lastAdd, doneTs)` as the recency signal — a bought-and-ticked item is quiet for the full tier threshold (10d frequency tier / 0.9×median-gap interval tier) regardless of how it got on the list; approximate by design (any edit to a done item refreshes the stamp) but errs toward suggesting LESS, never more; (2) the revive branch calls `addToHist(name, item.catId)` — a revive is a re-purchase, so it now feeds count/lastUsed/dates like a fresh add (count semantics now include revives, deliberate). **Known residuals (documented, not bugs):** the Home quick-pick and recipe re-tag revive paths still don't feed hist (data-quality only — chip suppression is handled render-side by (1) for every path); `addFromHistory` still has no revive branch at all (pre-existing v325 note). No data-model, export, or sync changes — `dates`/`count`/`lastUsed` already ride inside `hist` wholesale.
- **v341:** **Weight-change badge colours inverted — loss green, gain red (Cathal screenshot request 19/07/2026; first built as PR #95, which claimed "v340" in a same-day version collision with the chart-label fix that actually shipped as v340 via PR #96 — #95 was closed unmerged and its two-line payload rebuilt cleanly as v341).** The Track→Body weight-history diff badges (`.bw-entry-diff`, CSS ~line 501) used the generic up-is-green convention; for body weight, loss is the good direction. Two-line CSS swap (`.pos` now `#C04A5A` red, `.neg` now `#2F8A50` green — the same two colours, exchanged) with a comment marking the inversion as **deliberate** so a future session doesn't "correct" it back; the class names still describe the arithmetic sign of the change. The badge-builder JS, the BP traffic-light colours, and both v336 charts are untouched. Pure CSS — no data-model, export, or sync changes. **Process lesson from the collision: preflight should check open PRs for an already-claimed version number before picking the next one — two parallel sessions both took "v340".**
- **Fable sync/merge audit, no version bump (19/07/2026, same session as the design day below — deep read of the full sync surface: `mergeListItems`/`mergeHist`/`mergeBabyData`/`mergeTripsData`/`mergeMealPlanData`, the recipe merges, `pushPersonal`/`pushHousehold`, `applyPersonal`/`applyHousehold`, SSE listeners + echo handling + 5-min stale fallback).** **Two real defects found, both in the notes store — the one store the v245-class hardening never reached** (fix specs in the backlog's "Sync audit fix list"): **(1) Personal notes are WHOLESALE-REPLACED on the household channel** (`applyHousehold` ~10849 vs the personal channel's per-id union ~7052) — a device pushing a stale/partial copy guts the partner's notes, the exact class v245/v329 fixed for lists/baby (partial self-heal exists via the personal channel's union, but edits can still be lost since that union is remote-wins-per-id with NO recency check — an older backend copy overwrites a newer unpushed local edit on fetch); **(2) Work notes (`fl4_notes_global_work`) never sync AT ALL** — grep-verified present only in the NOTES_CONTEXTS config + export/backup; absent from every push payload and apply path on both channels, so Work notes are stranded per-device (backed up, not lost). **Latent class flagged:** `applyPersonal`'s workouts store rebuild (~7013, `Object.assign({}, remoteWD, {workouts,bodyweight,bp})`) drops any future local-only top-level `fl4_workouts` key when remote lacks it — the babySex incident class, inverse direction; harmless today (only the three arrays exist), fix the class when next touched. **Minor/accepted (documented, not queued):** no-recency merges on the personal channel (food_notes local-wins-per-key ~7078, workouts/bodyweight/bp local-wins-per-id ~7003 — cross-device edits of an EXISTING entry never land and the backend flip-flops by last pusher; adds/new entries are safe), `mergeHist` never reconciles counts (cosmetic suggestion-ranking drift), tie-break direction is inconsistent across merges (remote-wins: lists/medicine; local-wins: recipes/mealplan/bookings — local-wins ties are the safer default against v333-style echo strips), `pushHousehold` lacks pushPersonal's fetched-once gate (`_personalFetchOk`; mitigated by merge-on-receive everywhere except the notes defect above), duplicate `var lastPushTs` declarations (~1997 + ~6564, harmless). **Clean bills verified in code:** the v333 Firebase-normalisation echo class is closed everywhere else (swept every pushed payload for empty-object/empty-array/null stripping — all remaining cases guarded by `Array.isArray`/`||{}` or semantically equivalent); tombstone lifecycle consistent across all six namespaces (merge-before-apply, 90d purge, never exported); convergence re-push flags only fire when the merged set is genuinely fuller/newer — no ping-pong loop found; SSE reconnect + 5-min stale fallback + patch→refetch sound; 401 handling blocks stale personal pushes; test mode hard-blocks all pushes and SSE. App files untouched — the app stays v340.
- **Fable design day, no version bump (19/07/2026):** Cathal's Fable access ended that day, so the session was spent design-only per the 12/07 handoff policy — four backlog items settled and recorded as build-ready specs (all in the backlog below, each buildable by a plain-Sonnet session from the notes alone): **(1) sync hardening** — growth/milestones per-entry `updated` + tombstones fully specced (incl. the growth date-change/merge-key hazard); **scope correction: trip bookings were found ALREADY hardened** (`mergeTripsData` per-booking newest-wins + tombstones — the reviews' claim was stale) and teeth confirmed out of scope; **(2) back-button v2** — generic `data-bbclose` marker + one `closeTopOverlay` step covering all eight dynamic dialogs, plus trip-editor back-as-cancel (Cathal's call: ask "Discard changes?" first) and a `_tripEditing` stale-flag side-fix found during the design; **(3) add-to-grocery Phase 3 CLOSED** (Cathal heeded the dissent — decision record kept); **(4) baby growth centile chart** confirmed (weight-for-length centiles via real WHO LMS tables, replacing the v310 ratio chart; premise correction: the app had medians only, and no DOB exists — none needed). Housekeeping in the same pass: notes synced to v340 (entry added — it had shipped without a notes update), v330 detailed entry archived per keep-newest-~10. Same day, Cathal photographed the household medicine bottles, resolving council item ⑤: baby products are exactly the two already-confirmed ones (no gap); one tiny confirmed change queued — add `"panadol"` to the paracetamol advisory match list (adults use Panadol Extra Soluble; advisory-only, never a dose chip). App files untouched — the app stays v340.
- **v340:** **Fix: y-axis min/max value labels overlapping the x-axis date labels on the weight/BP/growth charts (PR #96, 19/07/2026).** The min/max value labels shared the same `x=padL` origin as the date labels with only ~4px vertical gap, so they visually collided at the bottom-left corner; the value labels moved to the left margin (`x=2`) in all three SVG chart builders (`growthRatioChartSvg`, `bodyweightChartSvg`, `bpChartSvg`). Pure SVG-render fix — no data-model, export, or sync changes. *(Superseded by the v349 chart redesign, which removed the min/max labels entirely.)*
- **v339:** **Nav consolidation Option B′ stage 1 — new "Track" section (Log · Food · Body) + dead duplicate Train block removed (Cathal asked to revisit Option B 19/07/2026; Fable design chat reshaped it to B′ once his actual pains surfaced — the general log and food log buried under Lists→Notes and weight/BP buried under Train were not things original Option B fixed; design confirmed via 3 AskUserQuestion answers: one Track section for all three logs · named "Track" · Track ships first; the full 7-expert council pass then ran pre-build per the 09/07 deferral condition — verdict build-with-changes, every condition adopted).** New top-level **Track** section (`trackSection`, nav `bnTrack` "Track" 📈 `&#x1F4C8;`, theme/active colour `#A05A10`) hosting three re-homed sub-views behind a config table (`TRACK_VIEWS`: view → container div + renderer — the council's config-driven-hosting requirement, extend this table rather than copying the pattern): **Log** (action log, was Lists→Notes→Log), **Food** (food journal, was Lists→Notes→Food), **Body** (weight+BP logging & the v336 charts, was Train→Body). **Pure re-hosting: the container divs (`notesLogView`/`notesFoodView`/`trainBodyView`) moved verbatim into `trackSection` keeping their pre-v339 ids, so `renderLogView`/`renderFoodView`/`renderBodyView` and all input wiring (`logActionBtn`, `foodAddBtn`, `bwLogBtn`, `bpLogBtn`, …) are untouched — zero data-store moves, zero sync changes** (`fl4_action_log`/`fl4_food_log`/`fl4_workouts` unchanged; the `fetchPersonal`-on-entry gate extended to `track` alongside `train`). Track is registered exactly per the v308/v338 section pattern: `switchSection` display/active-class/theme branch → `renderTrackView()`, nav click wiring, `HIDEABLE_SECTIONS` + `navIds` + Settings toggle row — **hideable, default ON** (a missing `fl4_secVisible.track` key resolves visible, so upgraded devices see it) — and inherits the v322 back-button section history for free. Notes is reduced to Personal/Work (Log/Food tabs removed; stale `currentNotesView` `"log"`/`"food"` guarded → `"personal"`). Train is reduced to Programs/Log/History — **Programs is the default view again** (deliberately reverses the v336 Body-first call, since Body left the section); stale `currentTrainView==="body"` guarded → `"programs"`; the Train sub-nav handler's `.tsn-btn` query scoped to `#trainSection` so it can't touch Track's tabs (which reuse the `.tsn-btn`/`.train-subnav` styling with `data-tview` attributes and an inline `#A05A10` background override). **Dead code removed (closes council fix-first item ④):** the fully-shadowed duplicate Train block (pre-v339 lines 8679–8977: first definitions of `renderWorkoutLog`, `_ssEi`/`openSetSheet`/`closeSetSheet`, a byte-identical duplicate `DOMContentLoaded` wiring listener, `renderWorkoutHistory`, `renderBodyView`, `renderTrainView`) — deleted before the Body move per the council order, and verified exactly one definition of each name remains.
- **v338:** **Family Activity Log v1 (backlogged 06/07/2026; design confirmed 18/07/2026 on Fable and built the same day on Cathal's go-ahead, PR #92).** New read-only, hideable **"Family Log"** section (`famlogSection`/`famlogContent`, nav button `bnFamlog` labelled "Family" 📖, active colour `#8A5A9A`) — **default hidden**, registered exactly per the v308 Sports pattern: `HIDEABLE_SECTIONS` + default-off in the `sectionVisible` IIFE, `navIds` map, Settings toggle row, `switchSection` display/active-class/title branch (sets themeColor + appTitle, calls `renderFamlog()`), nav click wiring — so it inherits the v322 back-button section history for free. **Pure render-time aggregation — zero new data model, zero new logging, no sync changes; the section never writes anything.** New top-level `famlogEvents()` + `renderFamlog()` (+ `_famlogDayStr`/`_famlogTimeStr` helpers, state `_famlogFilter`/`_famlogDays`), placed directly before `switchSection`. **Event sources:** baby medicine (`ts`; row "💊 name · dose · check the leaflet" with the dose time on the right — **factual only, all dosing/interval logic stays exclusively in Baby**), baby growth (`date`; "📏 Growth: X kg · Y cm"), baby milestones (user-typed `date`, falling back to the creation-time `id` day for undated ones), Train completed sessions (`date`; "💪 sessionName · N kcal"), trip boundaries ("🧳 Trip started" on `start` / "🏠 Trip ended" on `end`; rows deep-link into the trip detail via the Today-card `_tripOpenId`/`renderTripDetail` pattern), notes created (`createdAt` through `normaliseNotes`, both `NOTES_CONTEXTS` stores). **Excluded per the confirmed spec:** teeth (no dates stored), cardio, adult bodyweight/BP, sports, grocery/list adds. **Feed:** newest first, day-grouped via the reused Trips `dateSeparatorHTML`; whole-row tap deep-links via `switchSection` (notes rides the existing "notes"→Lists mapping); filter chips All/Baby/Train/Trips/Notes; 90-day window + "Load earlier" (+90 days per tap, button shown only while older events exist). One judgment call beyond the spec letter: **future-dated events are held back until their day arrives** ("story so far" — otherwise an upcoming trip's end date would sit at the top of the feed as "Trip ended"). All user strings go through `esc()`. **Baby-row wording council pass not yet run** — v338 ships only the pre-agreed factual medicine format; flagged in the PR for Cathal's call.
- **v337:** **Notes toolbar "?" legend (design confirmed 18/07/2026, built same day, PR #90).** A small **"?" button** (`neHelpBtn`) added at the end of the note editor's `TOOLBAR_HTML` (~line 5063) — not given the `.ne-fmt` class, so it's excluded from both the format-command click-wiring loop and its own legend listing. Tapping it opens a popover (`#neHelpPanel`, `role="dialog"`, absolutely positioned to cover the editor, inserted into `inner.innerHTML` between `#neBody` and the toolbar) listing every real toolbar button next to its name. The labels are read **live** from each button's own `title` attribute via `overlay.querySelectorAll(".ne-fmt")` inside a new `renderHelpList()` — per the confirmed design, the legend sources from the existing tooltips so it can't drift out of sync with the toolbar itself; no separate list to maintain. Toggle open/close via the "?" button or the popover's own ✕ (`neHelpClose`). **Back-button integration:** `closeTopOverlay()` (~line 7393) gained a new check ahead of the existing note-editor-close check — the legend popover closes first on back-press, the editor itself closes on a second press, consistent with the v330 popup-close-first pattern. No one-time tip, no localStorage flag, no sync — matches the confirmed design exactly. Pure UI addition — no data-model, export, or sync changes.
- **v336:** **Body weight & BP trend charts + Body-first Train sub-tab (design confirmed 18/07/2026, built same day).** **(1) Body promoted to the default/first Train sub-tab:** sub-nav button order reordered (Body, Programs, Log, History — was Programs, Log, History, Body); `currentTrainView` now initializes to `"body"`; `trainBodyView`'s static `display:none` removed and `trainProgramsView` gained one, so the section opens on Body without a nav-order change to any other section. **(2) Two hand-built inline-SVG charts**, following the existing `growthRatioChartSvg` pattern (polyline + dots + axis labels, no canvas/library) — rendered above the existing per-entry text lists (kept as-is, diff badges included), inside the live `renderBodyView` (~line 10717; the dead pre-v297 duplicate ~lines 8660–8761 was left untouched, confirmed via preflight). New top-level `bodyweightChartSvg`/`bwChartHtml` (single line, `fl4_workouts` `bodyweight` array) and `bpChartSvg`/`bpChartHtml` (systolic solid + diastolic dashed, sharing one mmHg y-axis). The BP chart's shaded background bands reuse the same traffic-light systolic thresholds as the BP history list (green &lt;130 / amber 130–139 / red &ge;140); since one y-axis can't band two readings' thresholds independently, the diastolic 80/90 thresholds are shown as dashed reference lines instead of a second band set — a reasonable rendering call within the confirmed design's intent, not itself part of the confirmed spec. Each chart needs &ge;2 points; below that it shows a placeholder instead of a broken/empty SVG. **Data/sync: no changes** — charts are render-only against the existing `fl4_workouts` store.
- **v335:** **Remove the orphaned pre-v318 per-list notes subsystem (scout-flagged 16/07/2026 during the back-button sweep; investigated and built 18/07/2026).** The scout flagged a possible signature-mismatch bug: `renderNotesIn`'s edit button called `openNoteEditor(lt, id, renderFn)`, but `openNoteEditor`'s real signature is `(title, body, onSave, onDelete)`. Tracing it further showed the whole call path is **unreachable, not a live bug** — `applyListTheme()`'s `currentList === "notes"` branch (the v318 comment: *"Notes is a sub-tab of Lists: it has no list machinery of its own"*) hides `#listContentWrap`/`#mainContent` (which contain the old `#tabNotes` tab and `#notesPanel`) in exactly the one state where `#tabNotes` would otherwise be shown, and shows the real `#notesSection` (`NOTES_CONTEXTS`-based Personal/Work/Log/Food) instead. This was a leftover pre-v318 implementation, already acknowledged as superseded by its own one-time migration code (`fl4_notes_notes` → `fl4_notes_global`, still present, untouched). Removed: the `#tabNotes` button, `#notesPanel` (`#noteInput`/`#addNoteBtn`/`#notesContent`), the static `#noteEditorOverlay` fullscreen-editor block, the functions `renderNotes`/`renderNotesIn`/`addNote`/`closeNoteEditor`/`openListNoteEditor` (the last three had zero callers even before this — `addNoteBtn` was never wired to `addNote` either), and their exclusive CSS (`.note-card`/`.note-text`/`.note-meta`/`.note-textarea`/`.note-edit-btn`/`.note-save-btn`/`.note-cancel-btn`/`.note-del`/`.note-rich`/`#noteEditorArea` rules — all verified zero remaining references before deletion). **Deliberately kept:** `getNotes(lt)`/`saveNotes(notes, lt)`, since the export map and import-merge code still call them directly for `fl4_notes_<lt>` — any pre-v318 per-list note data sitting in a device's localStorage keeps round-tripping through backup/export even though there's no UI to view/edit it directly any more. Zero user-visible change (the removed UI was never reachable); no data-model, export, or sync-contract changes. Scout finding (b) below is now resolved; finding (a) was folded into this same cleanup rather than done separately.
- **v334:** **Fix: unnecessary blank space at the top of the Notes tab (Cathal screenshot report, 17/07/2026).** Root cause: Notes is a Lists sub-tab (v318) — switching to it hides `#mainContent` but the div wrapping it, `<div class="content">` (padding `14px 14px 90px`, ~104px total), was never hidden itself, so it rendered as an empty padded box sitting above the real `#notesSection` UI (a separate sibling element later in the DOM). Fix: gave that wrapper `id="listContentWrap"` and added a third toggle in `applyListTheme()` (alongside the existing `notesSection`/`mainContent` toggles) hiding it whenever `currentList === "notes"`. Pure layout fix — no data-model, export, or sync changes.
- **v333:** **Fix: per-person packing circles reverting to a single checkbox ~1s after toggling on (Cathal's bug report, flagged at v332; root-caused and fixed on Fable 17/07/2026).** NOT the accepted v326 two-partner race, and not `mergeListItems`'s fault — a **Firebase data-normalisation echo**: Firebase Realtime DB does not store empty objects, so pushing an item with `checks:{}` ("opted in, nothing ticked" — exactly what the item-sheet toggle writes for a not-yet-packed item) comes back off the device's OWN SSE echo with the `checks` field missing but the **same** `updated` stamp; the merge's remote-wins-on-tie rule then deletes the field and `renderList` drops back to the single checkbox. Timing fits: 800ms push debounce + PUT + echo ≈ the observed ~1s. This breaks the `handleHouseholdEvent` design assumption that "our own echo is an idempotent no-op" — the echo is only idempotent when the pushed JSON survives Firebase unchanged. Ticking a circle directly (`togglePersonCheck`) never hit this: it always leaves ≥1 key and Firebase stores `false` values fine — only the *empty* object is stripped, which is why ticked circles survived while the fresh toggle-on reverted. **Fix (representational, no sync-machinery changes):** "opted in, nothing ticked" is now stored as explicit `{c:false,p:false,b:false}` — same truthiness, same auto-pack logic, survives the round trip. New top-level `emptyPersonChecks()` helper (beside `clearPersonChecks`) used at all four `{}` writers: the item-sheet toggle-on (which also now normalises a pre-fix persisted `{}` via an `!Object.keys(...).length` guard), `clearPersonChecks` (covers the three revive paths), Reopen-all's inline `{checks:{}}`, and `togglePersonCheck`'s init. The latter three were the same latent bug — an opted-in item surviving "Unpack all" or a revive would have silently lost its circles on the next echo. `mergeListItems`, the tie-break, and the push/apply paths are deliberately untouched: a merge-side patch can't distinguish a stripped `{}` from a genuine toggle-off deletion, so it would break opt-out propagation. Mixed-version note: a partner device still on ≤v332 doing "Unpack all" can still strip circles until it updates — self-heals once both sides are on v333.
- **Council whole-app review at v327, no version bump (16/07/2026):** Cathal asked the (reworked, 15/07) 7-expert council to review the latest version. **Verdict: ship / healthy — v327 stands as-is — with a fix-first list before the next feature version.** **(1) DOMPurify-missing fallback renders raw HTML** — the notes-body renderer (`bodyEl.innerHTML = (typeof DOMPurify !== "undefined") ? DOMPurify.sanitize(…) : (currentBody || "")`, ~line 5111 at v327) falls back to *unsanitised* `innerHTML` if the vendored `purify.min.js` ever fails to load; change the fallback to `esc()`/`textContent` — one line, the only XSS-shaped gap the review found. **(2) Per-entry union merge for the Baby MEDICINE array**, promoted out of the deferred nested-collection hardening: the "wait for a real dropped-dose incident" trigger can never observably fire (the interval advisory reads only *logged* doses, so a dose lost to a whole-collection remote-wins merge is invisible by construction); medicine entries already carry `id`+`ts`, so a medicine-only union is a much smaller job than the full hardening project — needs a Fable/Opus design chat first (sync + dosing categories). **(3) Widen the v326 `.ppc` per-person circles** — 24px visual + the `::before` halo gives only ~40×30px effective targets, three adjacent with 6px gaps; grow the circles (~28–30px + wider gaps) rather than the invisible halo (adjacent halos would overlap). **(4) Schedule the `openSetSheet`/`closeSetSheet` duplicate-block dedupe** (quirk #1) as its own micro-version the next time Train is touched, rather than leaving it to chance. **(5) Pending Cathal:** add the household's other *real* paracetamol/ibuprofen brand names to the `MED_RULES` match lists so hand-typed brands (e.g. Paralink) aren't silently untracked by the gap advisory — advisory tracking ONLY, never new dose chips (Healthcare seat's recorded condition: only Cathal-confirmed product names; the confirmed-strengths-only rule for dose chips is untouched). **Clean audit for the record:** every dosing non-negotiable verified in code at v327 (round-down to 0.25ml steps, 10ml cap, under-5kg Nurofen block, working + weight-date + "check the leaflet" wording, advisories never block logging); `esc()` correct incl. apostrophes (137 uses); DOMPurify vendored; v325/v326 field additions followed the export/merge contract correctly. **Dissent log:** Devil's Advocate wants a formal 2–3-version feature freeze, not just a fix-first list (the deferral-logic argument was adopted into item 2; the freeze itself is left to Cathal); Healthcare's condition on item 5 recorded above. **Open questions for Cathal:** which medicine bottles are actually in the house (unblocks 5) · the phone back-button test — open any notes/medicine sheet, press hardware back: does it close the sheet or leave the section/app? (decides whether the v322 back button needs a sheet-aware popstate guard) · whether the next version is a fix rather than a feature · ask Petra if ticks have ever vanished after simultaneous edits (would confirm/clear the accepted v326 checks race in practice). Items 1/3/4/5 are small and Sonnet-suitable; item 2 needs the design chat first. App files untouched — the app stays v327.
- **Council membership rework, no version bump (15/07/2026):** Cathal asked for a review of the `hearth-council` skill's seven seats; suggested changes applied to `.claude/skills/hearth-council/SKILL.md`. **(1)** PWA/Mobile UX engineer + Accessibility reviewer **merged** into one "Mobile UX & accessibility engineer" seat (their briefs overlapped heavily — both listed tap targets). **(2)** Firebase/sync architect **broadened** to "Sync & data-lifecycle architect": now explicitly owns export/import/cloud-backup coverage, `updated`-stamp discipline on mutation/revive paths, the babySex incident class, mixed-version convergence, and old-cache degradation — the app's most recurring bug class, previously uncovered. **(3)** Privacy advocate **broadened** to "Privacy & security reviewer": adds escaping (`esc()`/DOMPurify), Firebase rules, paste-import handling, and the third-party touchpoints (Open-Meteo, sports API, raw.githack). **(4)** New seat in the freed slot: **"Future maintainer"** — reviews designs for buildability/survivability in the single file (codebase quirks, new-field obligations, and whether the spec is tight enough for a plain-Sonnet build session per the 12/07 handoff policy); constructive counterpart to the Devil's Advocate, who is unchanged. **(5)** Stale facts fixed: 665k → ~715k, "6–8 icon nav" → 5-icon nav. Healthcare/dosing seat and parent-proxy seat unchanged. Council stays at seven experts; consensus rules untouched. App files untouched — the app stays v327.
- **v332:** **Confirm before "Clear completed/packed" or "Reopen all/Unpack all" (Cathal request, 17/07/2026).** Both bulk list-action buttons (`elClearBtn`/`elReopenBtn`, wired ~line 4703) now route through the same `confirmDialog()` pattern used elsewhere in the app instead of firing immediately on tap — a stray tap could previously wipe every done item or reopen/unpack the whole list with no undo. Messages are dynamic: count of affected items, and travel-specific wording ("packed"/"Unpack" vs "completed"/"Reopen"). The recipe-tag "Clear all" banner button (a different, unrelated control) already had its own three-way confirm dialog and was left untouched. Pure UI-safety change — no data-model, export, or sync changes. **Separately flagged, not yet fixed:** Cathal reported the v326 per-person packing circles reverting to a single checkbox ~1s after toggling them on for a Travel item (repro: item sheet → "Per-person checks" toggle on a not-yet-packed item), with household sync active. This looks like a sync/merge race (candidates: the 800ms-debounced `pushPersonal`/`pushHousehold` echo, or `mergeListItems`'s tie-break rule favouring remote on an equal `updated` timestamp) rather than a render bug, so per the model-check rule it's queued for a design/investigation session on Opus/Fable rather than guessed at here.
- **v331:** **Widen the `.ppc` per-person packing-check circles (council fix-first item ③, whole-app review at v327, built 17/07/2026).** The three per-person circles (C / P / 👶, travel items with "Per-person checks" opted in) only gave ~40×30px effective tap targets — the review's specific guidance was to grow the circles themselves rather than the invisible `::before` halo, since adjacent halos would overlap given the tight gap. `.ppc` grows 24px→30px (font-size 11px→12px to match), `.ppc-wrap` gap widens 6px→10px, and the halo inset (`-8px/-3px` → `-9px/-5px`) is tuned so adjacent halos meet exactly at the new gap boundary with no overlap. Effective tap target now ~40×48px, close to the ~44px named in the review. Pure CSS sizing — no render-function, data-model, or sync changes. Council fix-first item ③ struck below; ④ (`openSetSheet` dedupe, deferred to next Train touch) and ⑤ (`MED_RULES` brand names, blocked on Cathal) remain open.
- **v330:** **Back button closes an open popup first (council follow-up; design settled on Fable 16/07/2026, build Sonnet-suitable, built 17/07/2026).** The v322 `popstate` listener now calls a new top-level `closeTopOverlay()` FIRST, before ever switching sections; if it closed something, the pop is swallowed (`history.pushState({hearthSection: currentSection || "home"}, "")` restores the entry the pop consumed, forward-history loss accepted) instead of calling `switchSection`. **v1 priority order:** (1) `confirmDialog` — clicks `#_cfNo` if present (always Cancel, never OK); (2) note editor — if `#neBody` is in the DOM, calls `_neCloseFn()`, a new module global that `openNoteEditor` points at its own `doDiscard` closure on open and nulls on every close path (save/discard/delete) — so back-button close is the exact same code path as tapping the editor's own "‹" control, not a reimplementation; (3) static sheets by `style.display === "block"`: `#itemSheet`→`closeItemSheet()`, `#importSheet`→`closeImport()`, `#recipeImportSheet`→`closeRecipeImport()`, `#setSheet`→`closeSetSheet()`; (4) the shared `.overlay.open` group (weather/settings/sync/picker) — `closePicker()` for `#pickerOverlay`, otherwise `classList.remove("open")`; (5) `#toothModal.open` → class removed. **Housekeeping side-fix required by the build:** `closeImport()` was locally scoped inside a `DOMContentLoaded` closure (quirk violation — renderer-callable helpers must be top-level) so it couldn't be called from `closeTopOverlay()`; hoisted to top-level next to `closeItemSheet()`, nested duplicate removed, its one internal call site and `importLink` wiring resolve to the hoisted function unchanged. **Deliberately excluded from v1** (unchanged from the design spec): `#loginOverlay` (back must never dismiss login), the trip editor (`_tripEditing` — inline content replace, discards typed booking edits, revisit separately), and every one-off dynamic `role="dialog"` sheet (cloud backups, meal-plan picker/day sheet, sports manage, voice review, small prompt dialogs) — a v2 could close those generically only after verifying each one closes cleanly on backdrop click. No data-model, export, or sync changes. Struck from the backlog below.
- **v329:** **Baby sync hardening — household-channel per-entry merge + medicine `updated` stamps + medicine deletion tombstones (council fix-first item ②; design confirmed 16/07/2026 via 3 AskUserQuestion answers, all the recommended options: channel fix covers all four Baby collections · stamps/tombstones medicine-only · restore does NOT resurrect deleted doses).** **(1) One shared merge for both channels:** new top-level `mergeBabyData(local, remote, medTombs)` + `medUpdatedTs(m)` (beside the v245 tomb helpers) replaces BOTH the personal channel's inline `mergeByKey` block in `applyPersonal` AND the household channel's `Object.assign({}, getBD(), data["baby"])` in `applyHousehold` — the latter wholesale-replaced medicine/growth/milestones/teeth with the last-pushing device's copy, silently dropping e.g. a dose the sender hadn't received yet (the undetectable dropped-dose class the council promoted). growth (by date) / milestones (by id) keep remote-wins-on-same-key; teeth keeps the object union; the v296 generic carry-through for other keys (babySex class) is preserved inside the shared helper. **(2) Medicine newest-wins:** entries now stamp `updated: Date.now()` at log time and on the date/time edit; the merge keeps the newer copy per id (tie → remote, same rule as `mergeListItems`), reading `updated || id` — ids are `Date.now()` at creation, so **pre-v329 entries merge correctly with no migration** (same trick as v325's `itemAddedTs`). **(3) Medicine deletion tombstones (`fl4_tomb_med`, keyed by entry id):** the ✕ delete now calls `addTomb("med", id)` before filtering; tombstones ride both channels as **`med_deleted`** (naming matches `trips_deleted`) — always on pushPersonal, and on pushHousehold inside the `isSynced("baby")` gate but OUTSIDE the non-empty-arrays guard (deleting the LAST dose must still propagate); both receive paths merge+purge incoming tombstones BEFORE the baby merge. 90-day purge via shared `purgeTombs`; counted by the v299 sync-health panel for free (`fl4_tomb_` prefix); NOT exported (fl4_tomb_* policy). A new dose is always a new id, so **tombstones can never block fresh logging** — the v291 "never disable logging" rule holds trivially. **(4) Import:** `importBackupData`'s baby merge now filters incoming medicine through local tombstones — restoring a backup does NOT resurrect a deleted dose within the 90d window (confirmed choice, matches the v323 history rule; list items still resurrect per v288). **Mixed-version household:** a v328 device ignores `med_deleted` and still wholesale-replaces on receive, so it can briefly drop/resurrect entries locally — but every v329 device re-merges and re-filters on each receive and the old device recovers the full set on its next one; converges per-device, same rollout class as v245/v323. **Known accepted limits:** growth/milestones edits still have no per-entry stamps (remote-wins on same key, unchanged — backlogged in the sync-hardening entry); concurrent edits to the SAME dose within one sync window resolve newest-wins (older edit lost — fine for 2 users). Export machinery untouched — `updated` rides inside `getBD()` wholesale.
- **v328:** **Security fix — DOMPurify-missing fallback no longer injects raw HTML (council fix-first item ①, built same day, 16/07/2026).** The note-editor body setter (`bodyEl.innerHTML = …` in the editor-open path; preflight re-verified this is the file's ONLY DOMPurify call site) fell back to assigning `currentBody` raw into `innerHTML` whenever `typeof DOMPurify === "undefined"` — i.e. if the vendored `purify.min.js` (v289) ever failed to load (partial cache, corrupted fetch), stored or pasted note HTML would render unsanitised. The fallback is now `esc(currentBody || "")`: in that never-expected case the note shows as escaped plain text — ugly but safe; normal operation with DOMPurify present is completely unchanged. A comment at the site records the constraint so a future session doesn't "fix" the escaping back out. One-line code change + comment; no data-model, export, or sync changes. Fix-first item ① struck below — next council item is ②'s design chat (medicine-array merge, Fable/Opus).
- **v327:** **Weather rework — wind + compact layout on the card; popup slimmed to hourly + 3 upcoming days (Cathal request, 15/07/2026; iterated twice in-PR from his phone screenshots before merge — today's-average wind was built first, then reverted to current wind on review).** **(1) Card:** `wind_speed_10m` added to the `current=` params and stored as top-level **`wind`** (rounded km/h, current conditions) on the cached rec; rain and wind share ONE meta line ("☔ **N%** · 💨 **N** km/h"), and the "as of HH:MM" stamp moved out of the meta column to a new `#hwStamp` line under the temperature/condition (new static div + `.hw-stamp` CSS — the card's static shell is NOT rebuilt by a renderer, so a static child is safe here). Meta column is now 4 lines (Feels / H:L / rain·wind / Humidity), two shorter than v326. Deliberately NO wind fallback to `days[0].wind` for pre-v327 caches — that field is the daily MAX, not current; the wind part just hides until the first fresh fetch. **(2) Popup:** the `.hw-sheet-now` current-conditions header (icon/temp/cond/feels/humidity) is removed, CSS block included — the sheet is now hourly strip + upcoming-day rows only; the day loop starts at index 1 (today is covered by the front card and the strip's "Now" slot) with a `days.length > 1` guard; `forecast_days` bumped 3→4 (parse cap `i<4`) so three upcoming rows show (Tomorrow + two named days). **(3) Hourly strip:** `wind_speed_10m` added to the `hourly=` params, per-hour rounded `wind` stored, each hour chip gains a "💨N" line (`.hw-hour-wind`; always shown). Hourly rain keeps its ≥20% show threshold — confirmed with Cathal 15/07 that near-zero chances rendering blank is deliberate. `hwDayName`'s idx-0 "Today" branch is now unreachable from the renderer but kept. Old caches degrade gracefully (missing fields render as hidden/empty; the force-refetch on sheet open refreshes them). Device-local weather cache only — no data-model, export, or sync changes.
- **v326:** **Per-person travel packing checks (Cathal's idea, backlogged 03/07/2026; design confirmed 12/07/2026 via 3 AskUserQuestion answers, all the recommended options: 👶 emoji for the daughter's circle · auto-pack when all three are ticked · opt-in via a toggle in the item sheet).** Travel list items can opt in per item (shared items like sun cream stay on the single checkbox) via a new travel-only "Per-person checks" toggle in the item notes sheet (`#itemSheetPPWrap`, shown/hidden exactly like the Tags block). Opted-in items render three 24px circles — C / P / 👶 — in place of the single checkbox (`.ppc` CSS after the `.chk` rules; `cardHTML` branches on `item.checks`; new click zone 3a in the item-card dispatcher → top-level `togglePersonCheck(id, p)`). Ticking the third circle sets `done=true` (item moves to Packed, `today` cleared); unticking any circle in Packed brings it back — the circles ARE the done control for these items, no separate tick. **Data model:** one new optional per-item field, **`checks`** (`{c,p,b}` booleans); *presence* of the object = opted in, empty object = opted in with nothing ticked; the field is deleted when the toggle is turned off (done state kept as-is). Turning the toggle ON for an already-packed item pre-ticks all three so the states can't contradict. The three non-circle paths that force `done=false` (revive-on-re-add in `addItemToCurrent`, "Reopen all", the Home quick-pick revive) also clear the ticks via top-level `clearPersonChecks` — the two recipe re-tag revive paths were left alone deliberately (grocery-only, can never hold `checks`). **Sync/export: zero machinery changes** — `checks` rides inside the whole-item v245 union merge and the wholesale pushes; export/import/cloud backup untouched. The backlog's known race stands as accepted: two partners ticking *different* circles on the SAME item inside one sync window resolves whole-item newest-wins, so one tick can lose (just re-tick it). **Deliberate side-fix, flagged in the PR:** the item-sheet Save handler never stamped `item.updated`, so ANY sheet edit (name/notes/link/tags/amount — not just the new toggle) could silently lose the newest-wins merge to a partner's stale copy; it now stamps on save (same class as the v325 revive-path fix). **Housekeeping in the same pass:** the Sports fixtures backlog line (captured 03/07/2026) was still unstruck even though it shipped as v308 — struck now, nothing rebuilt.
- **Docs housekeeping, no version bump (12/07/2026):** full audit of CLAUDE.md / HEARTH-notes.md / HEARTH-archive.md (Cathal request). Fixed: critical quirk #1 rewritten — the "38 duplicate Train functions / expect a grep count of 2" warning was stale and misleading since the v297 dedupe (the one genuinely remaining duplicate, the `openSetSheet`/`closeSetSheet` block, is now what it flags); v322 given its own changelog entry (it was buried inside the v323 bullet with no version marker); Home-section description updated (v302 Today card, v322 clock, v316 town-name weather label were missing); detailed entries v308–v316 + the 05/07 tooling entry migrated to the archive per the keep-newest-~10 rule (archive header now v230–v316); struck-through done items pruned from the backlog (open items untouched); size figures refreshed to ~12,100 lines / ~715k at v326; notes workflow step 6 synced with CLAUDE.md's v324 test-link rule (it had drifted); storage-key/not-exported lists gained the missing device-local keys (sports, suggestion snooze, train-nav flag); archive v303 entry's stale "UI is v304" pointer corrected to v306; obsolete Project-knowledge upload instructions removed from the intro. App files untouched — the app stays v326.
- **v325:** **"🕒 Recent" filter chip on all four lists (Cathal request, backlogged 11/07/2026 via PR #70, built same day; design confirmed via 3 AskUserQuestion answers: all four lists · last-48h window · self-suppressing chip like ★ Today, all three the recommended options — "since I last opened the list" was considered and rejected as needing per-device bookkeeping with per-phone answers).** A "🕒 Recent (N)" chip renders at the top of every list (`#recentFilterChip`, teal #2D6B8A, own chip row below the ★ Today row on General) whenever anything was added in the last 48h; tap to show only those items, tap again (or switch list type — same `typeBar` reset as the other filters) to clear. Stacks with search/travel-tag/Today filters (applied last in the `renderList` chain) and reuses the Today chip's auto-disengage safety net so you can't be stranded in an empty filtered view. Count includes done items (an item added yesterday and already ticked is still "recently added"; it shows in its Done section while filtered). **Data model:** new optional per-item **`added`** ms-timestamp, stamped at all five item-creation sites (`addItemToCurrent`, `addFromHistory`, `addRecipeToGroceries` new-item branch, the paste-import new-item branch, Home quick-add) **and at all four revive-a-done-item paths** (same sites' restore branches) — a revived item counts as freshly added. Reader is top-level `itemAddedTs(it)`: `added` → else numeric `id` (ids have always been `Date.now()`-based, ≤1s jitter — so **pre-v325 items filter correctly with no migration**) → else `updated`. `RECENT_MS`/`isRecentItem` are top-level (renderer-called, quirk #6). **Sync/export: zero machinery changes** — `added` rides inside the whole-item v245 union merge, full-payload pushes, export/import and cloud backup untouched (list items travel wholesale; the trip-booking export map is a different rule and doesn't apply). Mixed-version household converges: a v324 device's pushes carry items wholesale so `added` survives round-trips; items *created* on the old device just fall back to id — same answer. **Deliberate side-fix, flagged in the PR:** the three revive paths that previously flipped `done=false` WITHOUT stamping `updated` (`addItemToCurrent` restore, both recipe/import re-tag un-done branches) now stamp it — before this, a partner's stale `done:true` copy could win the newest-wins merge and silently re-tick a just-revived item; Home quick-add already stamped it (the pattern followed). Known accepted quirk: `addFromHistory` has no revive branch at all (a done item re-added from History becomes a duplicate) — pre-existing, out of scope, left for its own fix.
- **v323:** **History cleanup — per-item + multi-select delete in the History tab, with sync-proof tombstones; grocery "Clear completed" removed (Cathal request, 11/07/2026; trigger: leftover test items polluting the v306 suggestion strip — his first instinct was to kill history entirely, corrected in design chat: suggestions AND the add-autocomplete read `hist` exclusively, done items are a separate thing that never feeds them).** **(1) History tab UI:** every row gains a ✕ "remove from history" button beside the existing + (32px circle, `.hist-del`, matching the v307 tap-target lesson); a new "☑ Select" toolbar button enters multi-select mode — tap rows to tick (`.hist-check` circles replace the +/✕), then "✕ Delete (N)" / Cancel in the toolbar (`histSelStart`/`histSelCancel`/`histSelDelete`, state in `_histSelectMode`/`_histSelected`, reset on tab entry via `switchTab`). Deliberately **no "clear all history"** anywhere — Cathal wants grocery history kept as a consistent base; deleting is item-by-item cleanup. Deleted entries drop out of the 🔮 suggestions and the add-box autocomplete immediately (both read `hist`). **(2) History tombstones (`fl4_tomb_hist_<lt>`):** reuses the generic v245 tomb helpers under a `hist_<lt>` namespace via new top-level `histTombKey`/`clearHistTomb`/`deleteHistEntries` — keyed by **lowercase item name** (hist entries have no id), 90-day purge via the shared `purgeTombs`, counted by the sync-health panel for free (same `fl4_tomb_` prefix). Pushed on BOTH channels as `histDeleted` alongside the existing `deleted`; both receive paths (`applyPersonal` + household) merge it via `mergeTombs` before the hist merge. `mergeHist` gained a third `histTombs` param and drops any entry whose tombstone is at/after its `lastUsed` (same rule as `mergeListItems`) — so re-adding an item revives it: `addToHist`/`addToHistFor` clear the local tombstone and stamp a fresh `lastUsed` that outlives the partner's tombstone copy. **NOT exported** (matches the fl4_tomb_* export policy), and `importBackupData` now passes local hist tombstones into its `mergeHist` call — **deliberate: restoring a backup does NOT resurrect deleted history entries** (within the 90d window); restored *list items* still resurrect per v288, unchanged. Mixed-version household: a v322 device ignores `histDeleted` and may re-push a deleted entry, but v323 devices keep filtering it via their tombstone — converges per-device, same rollout class as v245. **(3) Grocery "Clear completed" hidden** (one-line guard in `renderList`: `done.length && currentList !== "grocery"`): Cathal wants the grocery done list kept as a consistent record; per-item ✕ delete and "Reopen all" still work there, and todo/travel ("Clear packed")/personal keep their buttons unchanged.
- **v324:** **Pre-merge test links — "try this version" on every PR (Cathal request, 11/07/2026; design confirmed via 1 AskUserQuestion answer: instant raw.githack preview links chosen over Cloudflare Pages branch previews and over a second GitHub Pages repo — the latter shares the coshea321.github.io origin with the live app and would have needed riskier storage-separation code in the app itself).** Every PR body now STARTS with a "👉 Try this version" link (`https://raw.githack.com/coshea321/miniature-journey/<branch>/index.html`) so Cathal can tap it on his phone and exercise the change before merging (CLAUDE.md step 5 + `/release` step 7 updated to require it). Backing it is a new in-app **test mode**: `var _isTestBuild` (defined right after `_isPreview`) is true whenever the app runs on any hostname other than `coshea321.github.io` that isn't already a `_isPreview` context — fails safe on any unknown future host. In test mode: **(1)** fixed orange top strip `#testBuildBanner` "🧪 TEST BUILD — sync off · nothing here can touch the real app" (markup beside `#updateBanner`; shown by a boot check right after the `_isTestBuild` definition — the element sits BEFORE the main script so `getElementById` at script top is safe; `pointer-events:none` so it can never block taps). **(2) Firebase hard-blocked, defense-in-depth:** early-return guards in `getAuthToken` (always `cb(null)` → even if credentials somehow existed, no token is ever minted and the Firebase rules deny every read/write), `firebaseSignIn`/`firebaseSignUp` (cb "Sync is disabled in test builds"), `pushPersonal`, `pushHousehold`, `startPersonalListening`, `startListening`, `maybeCloudBackup`; `showLoginOverlay` suppressed, and the boot branch routes test builds straight to `switchSection` BEFORE the `isLoggedIn()` check so a stale/imported login state can't trigger `verifySession`. **(3)** Service worker never registered (both SW gates gained `_isTestBuild`) and the v312 boot splash hides immediately. **Isolation model (why this is safe):** browsers keep localStorage per-origin, so the test page's data is physically separate from the live app's even on the same phone; a test build starts empty — for realistic data, export a backup from the real app and import it into the test page (nothing can flow back: the guards hold even after an import, and exports carry no credentials). **Known limits, told to Cathal:** sync/login behaviour itself can't be exercised in a test build (still verified on the live app post-merge, as today); raw.githack.com is a third-party viewer of the already-public repo (it never sees Firebase data or credentials); all test links share the one raw.githack.com origin, so test data persists between test sessions until that site's browser storage is cleared. No data-model, export, or sync-protocol changes — every app edit is an early-return guard, one banner div, or the `_isTestBuild` block.
- **v322:** **Home-screen live clock + phone/browser back-button navigation (Cathal request, 10/07/2026; design confirmed via 2 AskUserQuestion answers: Ireland time shown only when it differs from local — i.e. auto-appears while travelling — and back button scoped to top-level sections only, not sub-tabs/modals).** **(1) Clock:** new `homeClock`/`homeClockIE` elements under the Home greeting/date; `updateHomeClock()` formats local time via `toLocaleTimeString("en-IE", {hour:"2-digit",minute:"2-digit"})` (matching the existing `en-IE` pattern used elsewhere) and the same call with `timeZone:"Europe/Dublin"` for home time — if the two formatted strings differ, a small "Home (Ireland) HH:MM" line appears, otherwise it's hidden. Driven by its own `_homeClockTimer` (15s interval, started/stopped via `startHomeClock()`/`stopHomeClock()` from within `switchSection`'s home branch/top) — **deliberately not reusing the shared Train-section `ST`/`clearST` timer**, which a Train timer stopping would otherwise silently kill. **(2) Back button:** the app had zero navigation history before this (`switchSection` was a pure in-memory `display:block/none` toggle, no `pushState`/`popstate` anywhere). `switchSection` now pushes `{hearthSection: section}` via `history.pushState` (or `replaceState` for the very first call, so boot doesn't leave an extra empty entry) whenever the section actually changes; a new top-level `popstate` listener reads the popped state and calls `switchSection()` again, guarded by a `_navFromPopstate` flag so the popstate-triggered call doesn't push a further entry (which would break forward/back). Scope is **top-level sections only** (Home/Lists/Train/Recipes/Baby/Trips/Sports) — Lists' History/Notes sub-tabs and modals like the Trip editor are unaffected and still use their own close controls. Works via Android's hardware back button in a PWA; iOS has no hardware back button, so there this only affects the in-browser back gesture. No data-model, export, or sync changes.
- **v321:** **Fix v320 trip deep-link — string/number id mismatch silently fell back to the trips list (Cathal-reported: "still not linking to the specific trip").** Root cause: `row.dataset.id` (the Today-card trip row's DOM `data-id` attribute) is always a string, but trip ids are created as numbers (`Date.now() + Math.floor(Math.random()*100000)`, the `tpSave` handler in `openTripEditor`); `renderTripDetail()` does a strict `x.id === _tripOpenId` lookup, so a string `_tripOpenId` never matched any trip and the function's own `if (!trip)` guard silently redirected back to `renderTrips()` — same end state as the pre-v320 bug, just via a different path, so it looked like the v320 fix hadn't landed at all. One-token fix: `_tripOpenId = Number(row.dataset.id)`. No data-model, export, or sync changes.
- **v320:** **Today-card trip row now deep-links to that trip, not the trips list (Cathal follow-up, 10/07/2026, small well-defined change).** The `renderTodayCard()` trip row names a specific upcoming trip with a countdown (e.g. "Paris in 3 days") but tapping it previously always reset `_tripView` to `"list"` and called `renderTrips()` — landing on the trips home page instead of the named trip. The row's `id: t0.id` is now carried through as a `data-id` attribute (escaped via `esc()`); the `.today-row` click handler's `act==="trips"` branch checks it and, when present, sets `_tripOpenId`/`_tripView="detail"` and calls `renderTripDetail()` directly, falling back to the old list behaviour only if no id is present. Section-switcher/sidebar navigation to Trips is untouched and still opens the list, which is the correct entry point there. No data-model, export, or sync changes.
- **v319:** **Sports homepage entry point (Cathal request, 10/07/2026; design confirmed via 1 AskUserQuestion answer: Today-card row, not a permanent tile).** Cathal had hidden Trips from the bottom nav via the Settings toggle and was relying on the existing "Today" summary card row (v302, `homeTodayCard`/`renderTodayCard`) — which shows an upcoming-trip line only when one exists — to open Trips instead. Sports had no equivalent, so hiding its (already-opt-in, default-off since v308) bar icon left no way back in short of Settings. Added a matching self-suppressing row: iterates `getSportsTeams()`, reads each team's cached next fixture (`sportsCache()[id].next[0]`, populated only once the Sports tab has been opened — same "device-local, pull-on-open" limitation as the rest of Sports), picks the soonest kickoff across teams, and shows "⚽ *Team* vs *Opponent* (H/A) · *when*" via the existing `sportsSummarise`; tapping the row calls `switchSection("sports")` (no extra render call needed — `switchSection` already re-renders Sports, unlike Trips which needs its list-view state reset). Suppressed entirely when no team has a cached fixture. No data-model, export, or sync changes — reuses `getSportsTeams`/`sportsCache`/`sportsSummarise` as-is.
- **v318:** **Nav consolidation — Option A (backlogged 01/07/2026; design confirmed 09/07/2026 via 2 AskUserQuestion answers: Option A over Option B now, and all three agreed stages shipped in one version rather than three).** Bottom bar 7 → 5: `Home · Lists · Recipes · Baby · Trips`. **(1) Notes is now the 5th sub-tab inside Lists** — new `data-lt="notes"` button on the typeBar shows the existing 4-tab notes section (Personal/Work/Log/Food, `renderNotesSection`) in place of the list UI; the Notes bar button, its `HIDEABLE_SECTIONS` entry and its Settings toggle are gone, and `switchSection("notes")` remains as a legacy deep-link shim (maps to Lists + notes sub-tab). Notes has no `LIST_CONFIG` entry, so `renderList()` gained an early-return guard — sync callbacks call it regardless of the active tab and it would otherwise crash on `LIST_CONFIG[currentList].key` — and `applyListTheme` hides the header item-count badge on the notes tab (same leak class as v314). The swipe-between-lists gesture picks Notes up automatically (it walks the visible typeBar buttons). Removed as dead code: the legacy `notesFullPanel` "global notes" view inside Lists + `renderNotesFull` (unreachable — no typeBar button ever selected it — and already broken: its + button had no listener; it showed the same `fl4_notes_global` store the Personal tab shows, so nothing is lost). **(2) Train is off the bar by default** (Sports precedent): default flipped in the `sectionVisible` init PLUS a one-off boot migration (`fl4_trainNavMigrated` flag) so devices that had stored `train:true` pre-v318 also start bare; the Home Training card stays the entry point (`switchSection("train")` works regardless of bar visibility) and the Settings toggle now means "pin Train back on the bar" (settings copy updated to say so). **(3) Trips header gained a "✈️ Packing" button** (the deferred v275 item) jumping to Lists → Travel by programmatically clicking the travel type-btn, reusing the full typeBar switch handler (search/filter resets, unseen clearing). No data-model, export, or sync changes anywhere in this version. Option B (thematic regroup) stays a future exploration — revisit only after Option A beds in. **Tooling in the same PR (Cathal asked about model economy):** new `.claude/agents/hearth-scout.md` — a **Sonnet, read-only code-location subagent** for "where is X / does X exist / who calls Y" sweeps, so expensive main-session models stop burning context on search output (preflight/verifier were already Haiku); CLAUDE.md workflow step 2 now points at it, with the explicit caveat that the main session must still read the exact lines it edits (the scout finds code, it doesn't vouch for understanding it — this session's `LIST_CONFIG` crash-guard catch came from direct reading). CLAUDE.md's model-check rule also gained the inverse direction: a Fable/Opus session that turns out to be doing a small well-defined change should say "this would have been fine on Sonnet" in the wrap-up so Cathal can calibrate session choices — never stopping work over it.
- **v317:** **Week plan — trimmed-down meal planner (from an external review's full-meal-planner suggestion, stripped per triage; design confirmed 09/07/2026 via 4 AskUserQuestion answers: household-shared · dinner-only, rolling next-7-days · lives inside Recipes · per-recipe Add-to-grocery kept unchanged).** "📅 Week plan" button at the top of the Recipes list opens a third recipe view: seven tappable day cards (Today / Tomorrow / "Thu 11"). Tap an empty day → bottom-sheet recipe picker (favourites first, fuzzy search); tap an assigned day → sheet with servings stepper / open recipe / swap / remove. **"🛒 Add week to groceries"** groups the week's assignments by recipe, SUMS servings across days (same recipe Mon+Thu at 4 each → one call at 8), and batch-calls the existing `addRecipeToGroceries` — zero new grocery/merge logic (new optional `quiet` third param suppresses its per-recipe toast; behaviour unchanged for all existing callers), and week-added items feed the v303/v306 replenishment history for free. Deliberately excluded, matching prior rejections: drag-and-drop (fiddly on touch), nutrition APIs / recipe photos (third-party dependency for a nice-to-have), lunch/breakfast slots (v1 is dinner-only). **New shared store `fl4_mealplan`** — one entry per `"YYYY-MM-DD"` day (`recipeId`, `servings`, `updated`, `addedBy`); the day string itself is the merge key: union of days + newest-wins by `updated` per day on BOTH channels with convergence re-push, **no tombstones** (clearing a day writes `recipeId:null` with a fresh stamp), entries >14 days old pruned on save/merge via a deterministic date cutoff so devices can't re-push-flap. Exported + daily-cloud-backed-up + import-merged (import reuses the same newest-wins merge, so restoring an old backup can't clobber a newer plan). Known deliberate limit: two *different* recipes sharing an ingredient already on the list re-tag it without summing amounts — that's Add-to-grocery Phase 3 (pack quantities), still on hold. New top-level: `getMealPlan`, `saveMealPlan`, `pruneMealPlan`, `mealPlanCutoffDay`, `setMealPlanDay`, `mergeMealPlanData`, `addWeekToGroceries`, `renderMealPlanner`, `mealPlanDayLabel`, `openMealPlanPicker`, `openMealPlanDaySheet`. (Naming note: distinct from `fl4_saved_meals`, the food-log's saved meals.)
- **v316:** **Weather card shows the actual nearby town instead of "Current location" (backlog item, captured 03/07/2026; design confirmed 09/07/2026 via 2 AskUserQuestion calls: pick this item over the other ready candidates, then the offline-table approach over a manual Settings field).** New `HW_TOWNS` table (~37 Irish towns/cities + approximate lat/lon) and `hwNearestTown(lat, lon)` — a plain Haversine nearest-match, no network call, no third-party geocoder. In `fetchWeather`'s success handler, when the device's own GPS coords were used (`isDevice` true), `rec.loc` is now `hwNearestTown(lat, lon) || "Current location"` — a real town name when within 20km of one of the table's entries, otherwise the previous generic fallback (e.g. abroad, or a rural spot away from any listed town). The non-device fallback path (`"Carrigaline"`, used when geolocation is denied/unsupported) is unchanged. Device-local only — weather is never synced, so no data-model/export/sync change.
- **v315:** **Recipes list opens on ★ Favourites by default (backlog item, captured 09/07/2026; design confirmed same day via 2 AskUserQuestion calls: fall back to All when no favourites exist, and only change the initial default — not a reset on every tab visit).** `_recipeFilter` (the module-level global driving the list's filter chip bar) now initializes to `"fav"` instead of `"all"`. A new guard at the top of `renderRecipes()`, right after `hasFav` is computed, resets it back to `"all"` for the session if no recipe is currently favourited — so the list never opens on an empty "Nothing here yet" screen with no Favourites chip visible to explain why (the Fav chip itself is only rendered in the bar when `hasFav` is true, same as before). Chip persistence for the rest of the session is unchanged: once you manually tap a different chip, it still sticks until reload, same as pre-v315 behaviour — this only changes the starting point. No data-model, export, or sync change.
