- **v314:** **Header item-count badge no longer leaks onto other sections (backlog item, captured 09/07/2026).** `switchSection` already hid `#hdrBadge` on every non-Lists section, but `renderList()` unconditionally re-showed it whenever it ran — so a background sync event (e.g. `fetchHousehold`/`fetchPersonal` callbacks, which call `renderList()` regardless of which section is on screen) could bring the badge back while Home/Baby/etc. was open. One-line guard in `renderList()`: the badge only shows when `currentSection === "lists"` (same `currentSection === "X"` idiom already used throughout the file, e.g. the list-swipe handler's `currentSection !== "lists"` guard). Notes currently lives as its own top-level section (`switchSection`'s `"notes"` branch, separate from `"lists"`) rather than as a Lists sub-tab, so no special-casing was needed there. No data-model or sync change.
- **v313:** **Weather card: High/Low and Rain% positions swapped (Cathal request, 09/07/2026).** The Home weather card's meta block now reads Feels → H:/L: → ☔ Rain % → Humidity → "as of" timestamp (was Feels → Rain → H/L since v305). One concatenation-order change in `hwPaint` — `popStr` moved after the H/L segment; no data, fetch, or cache changes. **Tooling in the same PR:** tightened `.claude/agents/hearth-verifier.md` check 3 after the v312 CI incident — the exactly-one-version-string check is now explicitly zero-judgement (a second `vNNN · DD/MM/YYYY` match FAILs even when it's "only" a code comment; in v312 the verifier waved exactly that through and the GitHub Action then red-✗'d the PR). Code comments must use the placeholder form `vNNN · DD/MM/YYYY`. Also noted for the record: v312's post-merge GitHub Pages deployment failed transiently (known GitHub flake) and succeeded on the next push to main — no action ever needed beyond re-triggering/waiting.
- **v312:** **Cold-start opening-flash fix — boot splash + version gate (Cathal backlog item 03/07/2026; design confirmed 06/07/2026: "smart" 4s→8s wait budget + dark splash matching the Android launch screen).** A full-screen `#bootSplash` overlay (inline-styled, first element in `<body>`: `#1C0A04` background + app icon + pulsing dots) covers the app from first paint; a new boot gate in the SW section of the script decides when it's safe to reveal. **Zero sw.js logic changes** (VERSION bump only) — the gate reuses the existing `GET_VERSION`/`SW_VERSION`/`SW_UPDATED` postMessage plumbing. Hide-immediately paths: `_isPreview`, no SW support, no `navigator.serviceWorker.controller` (first visit / hard refresh — the page came straight from the network), SW registration/update failure, offline. Otherwise a 4000ms deadline runs, extended once to 8000ms total (splash shows "Updating…") when a new SW is actively installing (`updatefound`/`reg.installing`). `PAGE_VERSION` is captured from the static `#appVersionLabel` text at gate setup, before any SW message overwrites the label — so **no new version-bump site**. Versions compare **numerically** via `_verNum` ("v312 · date" → 312): SW newer than page → the fresh shell is already in the SW cache → one **silent reload behind the splash** (`bootStaleReload`, sessionStorage `hearthBootReloaded` loop guard; a second attempt in the same session falls back to hiding the splash + the banner). Page newer than SW (fast network served fresh HTML while the old SW was still active) → keep waiting for `SW_UPDATED` to confirm, NO reload — **a plain `!==` comparison here would have wasted a reload on every good-network update open; caught during build.** Versions equal + `reg.update()` settled + nothing installing → confirmed current, splash fades (~180ms) and the loop-guard flag clears. Mid-session updates after the splash hides keep the existing tap-to-refresh banner, now improved: `SW_UPDATED` only shows the banner when the activated version actually differs from the page (previously a spurious "Update available" could appear when the page already WAS the new build). Worst case (update can't finish inside 8s on a bad connection) is unchanged from today — old version + banner — just behind a longer splash. The v295 lie-fi 3.5s shell race and all offline behaviour are untouched.
- **v311:** **Fix Sports section "Maximum call stack size exceeded" crash (Cathal-reported bug).** Root cause: `fetchSportsForTeam` synchronously invoked its callback (`renderSports`) whenever a fetch for a team was already in flight; `renderSports` unconditionally calls `refreshSports(false)` at its end, which — since the team's cache still wasn't populated (the original fetch hadn't resolved) — called `fetchSportsForTeam` again, hitting the same synchronous-callback branch again, and so on, an unbounded synchronous recursion with no async boundary to break it. Triggered by re-rendering/reopening the Sports tab while a team's fetch was still pending (e.g. slower network, quick tab switch away and back). Fix: the already-fetching branch now just returns without calling back — the in-flight fetch's own `step()` already calls `cb()` once when it resolves, so a concurrent caller doesn't need (and must not trigger) another render. One-line change in `fetchSportsForTeam`, no data-model or UI change.
- **Tooling, no version bump (05/07/2026):** **Workflow automation.** Added `.claude/agents/hearth-preflight.md` (Haiku subagent, run BEFORE editing: confirms base = latest origin/main, pushes `backup-vNNN`, reports version state, greps for already-built features, lists backup branches outside the keep-10 window) and `.claude/agents/hearth-verifier.md` (Haiku subagent, run AFTER every edit: `node --check` on the extracted main script block, file-integrity checks, version-bump consistency; verdict SAFE TO COMMIT / DO NOT COMMIT). Added `.claude/commands/release.md` (`/release`: bump both files, update notes, verify, commit, push, open the PR), `.claude/skills/hearth-council/SKILL.md` (full 7-expert council spec, loaded only on "use my council"), and `.github/workflows/hearth-pr-checks.yml` (zero-token CI backstop running the verifier's mechanical checks on every PR; to make it merge-BLOCKING Cathal must add a branch-protection rule on `main` requiring the "Hearth checks" status — until then a failure just shows a red ✗ on the PR). **Split this file:** detailed changelog v230–v298 moved to `HEARTH-archive.md` (~101k → ~52k chars read per session; read the archive only when an old version is referenced). CLAUDE.md workflow slimmed to delegate steps to the two subagents. Also added the **model-check rule** to CLAUDE.md (Cathal's request): sessions must state their model and prompt him to restart on Fable/Opus before building anything risky (sync, dosing, trip import/export, service worker) or design-fuzzy; plain Sonnet stays the default for small well-defined changes. App files untouched — the app stays v308.
- **v310:** **Growth chart redesigned to one weight/height-vs-WHO-median ratio chart (Cathal follow-up on v309, same day).** Replaced the two separate Weight/Height mini-charts with a single chart: the ratio of logged weight to the WHO weight-for-length median for that reading (the same ratio `growthBand()` already uses for the per-entry Below/Normal/Above label), plotted as a **percentage** over time, with a **shaded "Normal" band (90–110%)** and a dotted 100% line marking the WHO median itself. Design confirmed first (2 AskUserQuestion calls): percentage y-axis (not raw decimal ratio) and reuse the existing 90/110 thresholds rather than picking new ones. Refactored `growthBand()`'s internal ratio calc to percentage and pulled the thresholds out into shared top-level constants `GROWTH_BAND_LO`/`GROWTH_BAND_HI` (90/110) so the list labels and the chart band can never drift apart. Replaced `growthChartSvg()` with `growthRatioChartSvg(points, color)` (draws the band rect + median dotted line + ratio line/dots) and rewrote `growthChartsHtml(bd)` to build the one chart from entries with both weight+height logged. Needs `babySex` set (the ratio needs a WHO median lookup) — shows an explanatory placeholder if not, or if fewer than 2 qualifying readings exist yet. No data-model, export, or sync changes.
- **v309:** **Visual growth chart (backlog item, picked up 05/07/2026).** The plotted SVG "trend line" Cathal originally pictured, alongside the v301 text band-trend (kept as-is). Two stacked mini line-charts in the Growth view — **Weight** and **Height** — sit right below the band-trend summary and above the measurement list. Design confirmed first (3 AskUserQuestion calls): both metrics charted (not a toggle), a dotted WHO weight-for-length **median overlay** on the weight chart only (computed per-point via the existing `growthMedianForLength`, since that's the only WHO table in the app — no equivalent height-for-age table exists), placed above the list rather than below it. New top-level `growthChartSvg(points, medianPoints, color, unit)` (hand-built inline SVG — no charting library, single-file app) and `growthChartsHtml(bd)` (builds both charts from `bd.growth`, ascending by date; line colour follows `babySex`, girl/boy/neutral). Each metric independently falls back to a "log a couple more readings" placeholder below 2 points (a line needs ≥2 to mean anything); the whole section is skipped when there are no measurements at all (the existing "No measurements yet" empty state still covers that). Chart section only appears in the Growth view — no data-model, export, or sync changes.
- **v308:** **Sports section — hideable pull-on-open scoreboard (Cathal request; design + API verified first).** New 8th nav section 🏅/⚽ **Sports**, **default HIDDEN** (opt-in via Settings section toggle, so it doesn't add an 8th icon for anyone who doesn't want it — Cathal enables it). Per configured team: **next fixture** (vs opponent, H/A, local kickoff time, competition) + **last result** (W/L/D + score). Data from **TheSportsDB free key "3"** — Cathal verified in his browser that `eventsnext.php` returns fixtures on the free tier (the patron-tier risk). Endpoints: `eventsnext.php?id=`, `eventslast.php?id=` (returns `results` or `events`), `searchteams.php?t=` (config). **Device-local, NOT synced/exported:** `fl4_sports_teams` (list; seeds with Man Utd 133612 until edited) + `fl4_sports_cache` (per-team next/last + `at`). Refresh on tab open, cache TTL 2h, offline shows cache. **Manage teams** modal: search by name → add, × to remove. **Hard limits (by design, stated in-card "updates when you open this tab · not live"):** no live scores, no alerts (push is ruled out). Coverage reality (told Cathal): soccer/national teams good (Man Utd, Inter Miami, Argentina, Ireland); GAA + golf NOT covered by the free tier and out of scope. New top-level: `getSportsTeams`/`saveSportsTeams`, `sportsCache`/`saveSportsCache`, `sportsWhen`, `sportsSummarise`, `fetchSportsForTeam`, `refreshSports`, `renderSports`, `openSportsManage`. Wiring: nav button `bnSports`, `sportsSection`, `HIDEABLE_SECTIONS`+sports (default false), `applySectionVisibility` navIds, `switchSection`, settings toggle `data-sec="sports"`. Cross-origin API → SW fetch handler ignores it (same-origin only); no SW/caching change. **First non-utility (leisure) feature + first standing third-party data dependency beyond weather — accepted; free-API longevity is a known risk.**
- **v307:** **Suggestion chip tap-target fix + empty-list bug (Cathal: v306 add/dismiss too small & close).** (1) Rebuilt each grocery-suggestion chip as **two large, divider-split targets**: the whole left block (+ · name · reason, ~40px, `padding:9px 13px`) adds; the right × (own ~40px block, `border-left` divider) dismisses. Removed the cramped tiny-+-beside-tiny-× so they can't be mis-tapped. (2) **Bug found while testing:** the v306 strip was injected only in the populated-list path, so it never showed on an **empty** grocery list — exactly when suggestions are most useful. `suggHtml` now computed before the empty-list early-return and prepended there too; chip wiring extracted to shared `wireSuggestionChips()` called from both paths.
- **v306:** **Predictive replenishment suggestions UI — step 2 of 2 (visible half of the v303 foundation).** A "🔮 Suggested" chip strip at the **top of the grocery list only** (suppressed while searching). Two tiers from the add-date history: **interval-based** when an item has ≥3 recorded `dates` (median gap → suggest when `daysSince >= gap*0.9`, reason "usually ~Nd"); **frequency fallback** (`count>=3` and not added in 10+ days, reason "often bought") so it's useful immediately before the v303 date series fills in. Excludes items already on the list; max 4, ranked by score (interval matches outrank frequency). **Never auto-adds** — tap a chip (or its +) to add via the shared `addItemToCurrent`, tap × to **snooze that item 14 days** (`fl4_sugg_snooze`, device-local + transient, NOT exported/synced). New top-level: `buildGrocerySuggestions`, `buildSuggestionsStrip`, `medianGapDays`, `suggSnoozeMap`/`suggSnooze`; injected in `renderList` as `suggHtml + html` with chip wiring alongside the existing handlers. Grocery-only by design (replenishment cadence is a consumables concept; todo/travel don't fit). **This completes unrequested feature #1** (foundation v303 + UI v306); both unrequested features now shipped (voice = v304).
- **v305:** **Today's chance of rain on the weather card (Cathal request).** The Open-Meteo fetch already requested `precipitation_probability_max` (stored as `days[i].pop`); now also stored top-level as `rec.pop` (today's max) and shown on the main weather card's meta block as "☔ Rain N%" (after Feels, before H/L). `hwPaint` prefers `data.pop`, falls back to `data.days[0].pop` for caches written before v305. Shown only when available (`pop != null`); no new API call. Trivial surface-existing-data change.
- **v304:** **Voice capture — speak a list, get items parsed & added (unrequested feature #2; design-confirmed: Google transcription OK, grocery/todo/travel/personal only NEVER medicine, multi-item split yes).** 🎤 mic button on the list add row (`itemInput`), shown only when `voiceSupported()` (Web Speech API present — feature-detected, hidden on unsupported browsers e.g. some iOS). Tap → `startVoiceAdd()` (lang en-IE, single utterance; tap again to stop) → `parseVoiceItems()` splits the transcript on commas/"and"/"then"/"plus"/";"/newlines, trims, de-dupes, capitalises, caps 20 → **always** shows `showVoiceReview()` modal (heard-transcript + removable chips + "Add to <list>" / Cancel) so a mis-hear is never silently committed. Confirmed items add via the new shared **`addItemToCurrent(name,catId,dueD,restoreCat)`** core extracted from `doAdd` (both now share it; `doAdd` behaviour preserved except new-item id gains a `+random` suffix so rapid multi-adds don't collide). Persists once via `saveCurrentList()` (which already pushes both sync channels). **Privacy:** on Android the Web Speech API transcribes via Google (same as the keyboard mic) — Cathal accepted; audio never touches Firebase/synced data, transcription only. **Safety:** scoped to `itemInput` which only ever serves grocery/todo/travel/personal — the medicine input (`medName`) is a separate field and is deliberately untouched. **Known caveat:** the "and" split can over-split compound names ("fish and chips" → two chips) — the review step lets the user drop them. New CSS `.voice-mic-btn` + `.listening` pulse.
- **v303:** **Predictive-replenishment DATA FOUNDATION (invisible; step 1 of 2 for the unrequested "smart suggestions" feature Cathal green-lit).** No user-facing change. `addToHist`/`addToHistFor` now record a rolling **`dates` array** (add-dates, `"YYYY-MM-DD"`, deduped per day, capped at 8 → up to 7 intervals) on each grocery/list history entry, via a shared new helper `histRecordDate(entry)`. This is the prerequisite the cadence prediction needs — you can't predict "usually every ~5 days" without recording when things are added. Purely additive: rides through export/import/sync inside the existing `hist` payload with **zero** changes to those paths (verified: `mergeHist` keeps whole entries union-by-name, list push/import carry `hist` wholesale). Backward-compatible — `dates` is absent on pre-v303 entries until their next add; all readers must treat it as optional. **Deliberately ships invisible so the cadence data starts accumulating now; the visible "Suggested for your list" UI landed as v306 (v304 ended up being voice capture) after its own design chat (placement, immediate count+lastUsed "buy again" vs interval-based "due now", thresholds, dismissal, never auto-add).** Known limitation (accepted): household hist merge is union-by-name newest-entry-wins, so two devices' `dates` for the same item don't union — approximate cadence, fine for suggestions.
- **v302:** **"Today" summary card on Home (Tier 2 item 4 — the last Tier 2 feature; the aggregator all four reviews wanted).** Compact card at the top of Home (above the existing Train/Baby stat grid, below weather; existing spotlights untouched). One tappable line per domain, each **self-suppressing** when it has nothing to say: 🛒 groceries to buy · ⭐ tasks starred for today · 💪 workouts done today (positive only — no nag when absent) · 👶 last medicine *if logged today* (informational, reuses last-med line, NOT the dosing-safety logic) · ✈️ nearest upcoming trip countdown. When all lines are empty → "✨ All caught up for today". Each row jumps to its section (lists set currentList + switchTab, others switchSection). New `renderTodayCard()` + `_tcIsToday()`; extracted `tripCountdownLabel(t)` from `tripCountdownBadge` (v298) for reuse. Deliberately excluded: notes (no done-state → raw count, low value) and weather (own card). Reviews framed it as "Today assistant" — built as an honest self-suppressing summary, no scheduling/AI. **This closes the full Tier-2 queue; the visual growth chart remains optionally on the backlog per Cathal (v301 shipped the text band-trend, he may want a plotted SVG chart later).**
- **v301:** **Growth-trend summary (Tier 2 item 3 — "percentile trend"; design-confirmed first).** SAFETY-SENSITIVE (child health). **Key honesty decision, agreed with Cathal:** the app only holds WHO weight-for-length *median* values (`WHO_WFL`), NOT the percentile spread (LMS tables), so a specific percentile number ("45th percentile") is **not computable and must never be shown** — that would be fabricated precision. Instead: an honest **band-trend** line at the top of the Growth view — current weight-for-length band ("around average / a little below / a little above" for length) plus direction over the last up-to-3 readings ("holding steady / drifting up / drifting down", threshold |Δratio| < 0.03). Reuses the existing band data; carries the same "weight-for-length vs WHO median · not medical advice" disclaimer inline. New `growthTrendSummary()` + extracted shared helper `growthMedianForLength(h,sex)` (median interpolation, now used by both `growthBand` and the trend so the calc isn't duplicated). Needs `babySex` set + ≥1 record with weight+height in the 45–110 cm range; direction only shown with ≥2 qualifying readings. Uses `her`/`his` per `babySex`. Placement: Growth view only (Baby-strip surfacing declined). Reviews' framing was "you have WHO curves, add percentile" — corrected: only medians present, so band-trend is the honest version.
- **v300:** **Autofill/password-manager misfire fix (Cathal reported: tapping random fields sometimes prompted to save/choose a username+password).** Root cause = phone browser password manager, not an app bug, but Hearth armed it: three `type="password"` fields (login password + two "API key" fields) with no `autocomplete` identity meant the browser scanned the page and heuristically paired unrelated text fields with the password box. Fix: gave the **real** login pair explicit identity — `loginUsernameInput` → `name="username" autocomplete="username"`, `loginPasswordInput` → `name="password" autocomplete="current-password"` — so the browser knows exactly where the login is and stops guessing elsewhere; and marked the two API-key fields (`loginApiKeyInput`, `syncApiKeyInput`) `autocomplete="off"` + `autocapitalize/autocorrect/spellcheck` off so they stop arming the credential manager. Pure attribute change, no JS/behaviour touched. (If a *specific* field still misfires, add `autocomplete="off"` to that one — ~20 text inputs across the app lack it but weren't churned since the login-pair fix is the actual lever.)
- **v299:** **Sync-health panel in Settings (Tier 2 item 2; suggested by reviews 3 & 4).** New "📡 Sync health" button (under Cloud backups) opens a read-only diagnostics dialog: Status (signed in / logged out / offline), This device (online/offline via `navigator.onLine`), Household (paired / this-device-only), Live updates per channel (personal + household EventSource `readyState` → live / connecting / reconnecting / off), Last sent up, Last received, Pending deletions (total tombstone count across all `fl4_tomb_*`), Last cloud backup. A "Sync now" button force-fetches+pushes both channels and reopens the panel after 1.2s. **New observational state `_syncStats` {lastPersonalPush, lastHouseholdPush, lastEvent}** — set on the actual successful PUT (`.then(r=>r.ok)`) in `pushPersonal`/`pushHousehold` and at the top of both SSE handlers; nothing depends on it, it's display-only. New helpers: `syncAgo` (reuses `medAgoStr`), `countAllTombs`, `esState`, `openSyncHealthSheet`. Dialog at z-index 890 (same as cloud-backups sheet, below confirmDialog 900). No data-model/sync-behaviour change.
- **v298:** **Trips departure countdown badge (Tier 2 item 1 — the v275-deferred "countdown to departure").** New teal pill on each trip card: "Today" / "Tomorrow" / "In N days" for upcoming departures, "Now" while a trip is in progress (start passed, end still future), nothing for past trips. Two new top-level helpers: `tripDaysUntil(dateStr)` (whole-day delta from local-midnight today to a "YYYY-MM-DD" date, DST-safe because both endpoints are local midnight; null if unparseable) and `tripCountdownBadge(t)` (returns the pill HTML or ""). Wired into `tripCardHTML` as a flex sibling of the name/date block, so it shows in both Upcoming and Past sections but self-suppresses for past trips. No data-model change — reads existing `t.start`/`t.end`. Home dashboard "next departure" line NOT done here (that's part of the Tier 2 Today-card work).
- **v297:** **Train duplicate-function dedupe (external-review Tier 1, item 3 — closes the Tier 1 plan).** The seven remaining top-level Train helpers defined TWICE (`trainTodayStr`, `trainShiftDay`, `trainFmtDate`, `trainFmtDateLong`, `timerCircle`, `wireInputs`, `wireWorkoutLog` — remnants of the old "38 duplicates" incident) each had their **first** copy deleted. Method: every pair was extracted and diffed first — all seven byte-identical — and since both copies were top-level in the same script scope, function-declaration hoisting means the second always won at runtime, making the first copies provably dead. Zero behaviour change by construction; the trap it removes is "edited the first copy → silent no-op". `doSave` ×3 deliberately kept: those are nested closures in three different editors, not duplicates. ~78 lines deleted.
- **v296:** **Field-wise sync merges — Baby + Trips (external-review Tier 1, item 2).** Closes the "hand-typed field list silently wipes fields it doesn't name" class (the old babySex bug). (1) **applyPersonal baby merge:** known collections keep their existing union semantics (growth by date, medicine/milestones by id, teeth object-merge, bags remote-wins); every OTHER key now carries through generically — local wins when set (non-empty), remote fills gaps. (2) **applyHousehold baby:** was a **wholesale replace** (worse than the review thought — local unpushed baby changes could be dropped entirely); now `Object.assign({}, local, remote)` — remote still wins for every key it carries (household deletion semantics unchanged) but local-only keys survive. (3) **mergeTripsData:** trip-level merge now builds from both copies (winner's keys win, loser fills gaps) instead of a hand-typed `{id,name,start,end,updated}`; booking-level newest-wins now fills keys that are **undefined on the winner** from the loser — so an older-app-version device editing a booking can no longer wipe fields it predates (boarding/gate/seats being the live example). Deliberately `undefined`-only: a cleared field is `""` (defined) and stays cleared. **Known remaining limitation (accepted, needs baby tombstones to fix properly):** concurrent edits to the same baby COLLECTION across the household channel still resolve remote-wins (e.g. a dose logged locally in the window before a partner push could be dropped from the household copy, though the personal channel's union usually self-heals it). Documented, not fixed here.
- **v295:** **SW "lie-fi" fix + tombstones 90d (external-review Tier 1, item 1).** The v286 network-first shell had no timeout: on a connection that's present but not passing data, the app sat near-blank until the browser's own network timeout (up to ~60s) before cache fallback. Now the shell fetch races a **3.5s timer** — network wins if it answers in time (fresh-on-open behaviour unchanged), otherwise the cached shell is served and any late network response still lands in the cache for next open; with no cached copy at all (first ever visit) it keeps waiting on the network as before. Tombstone purge extended 30d → 90d (drawer-bound-tablet scenario; tombstones are tiny).
- **v294:** **Nav cruft cleanup — dormant Baby list + test lists removed (Cathal confirmed: his baby-list items were stale, the Baby section's Bags list is current).** Deleted: the "🍼 Baby list" list type (`LIST_TYPES.baby`, `LIST_CONFIG.baby` with key `fl4_babylist`, `BABY_CATS`, `BABY_KW`) and both hidden test types (`testdue`, `testgrocery` + their invisible tab buttons + the onLoginSuccess hide code + ~8 `currentList === "testdue"`-style conditionals). The list-type iteration loops went from 5 entries to 4 (`["grocery","todo","travel","personal"]`) in **10 places**: buildExportPayload ×2, importBackupData ×3 (lists/notes/customCats), pushPersonal, applyPersonal, pushHousehold, renderCustomCatsList ×2 — plus clear-all. `applyHousehold` already excluded it. **Deliberately kept:** the Settings sync toggle `data-key="baby"` and `isSynced("baby")` — they gate the **Baby SECTION** data (`bd`: growth/medicine/milestones/teeth/bags), which is completely separate from the removed list; same for the `babySection`/`bnBaby`/`renderBabyView` references. **Data notes:** old `fl4_babylist` / `fl4_testdue` / `fl4_testgrocery` localStorage keys become inert orphans on existing devices (not migrated, not deleted); Cathal's stale baby-list items remain recoverable from pre-v294 export files and cloud backups if ever wanted; importing an old backup file that contains `lists.baby` now silently ignores that block; the remote `list_baby` node disappears from the personal doc on the next full PUT (harmless).
- **v293:** **Seat(s) field on flights** — third optional flight field `seats` (free text, e.g. "14A, 14B"), same pattern as v292's boarding/gate: editor input in `bkFlightFields` (full-width, under the Boarding/Gate row) + same field in the connecting-leg section (`bkSeats2`); the collapsed card's 🛫 line now joins Boarding · Gate · Seats with only the parts that are set. All three data-model touchpoints updated (export map, `importTripFromJSON`, `mergeBookingsIntoTrip`, fallback `""`) + CLAUDE.md field list.
- **v292:** **Trips: boarding details + one-step connecting flights (Cathal's request; design confirmed via 2 AskUserQuestion calls — time+gate and new-bookings-only both accepted).** (1) **Boarding time + gate on flights:** two new optional booking fields, `boarding` ("HH:MM" from a time input; date assumed = departure date) and `gate` (free text, add-on-the-day). Editor shows them only when type=flight (`bkFlightFields`, toggled by the type select). The **collapsed** card gets a second line "🛫 Boarding **14:20** · Gate B23" — deliberately not behind the expand, since it's the at-the-airport line; renders nothing when both empty. **All three data-model touchpoints updated in the same change** (export map in tpExport, `importTripFromJSON`, `mergeBookingsIntoTrip`, fallbacks `""`) + CLAUDE.md field list. (2) **Connecting-leg entry (NEW flight bookings only):** "⇄ Add connecting leg" button reveals a compact leg-2 section (title, start, boarding/gate, location); one Save creates BOTH bookings — leg 2 as a separate booking with `connectsFrom:true` and **leg 1's ref** — so the data model, sync merge, tombstones and the existing "⇄ Transfer · Xh Ym" timeline display are all untouched. Leg 2 validates before leg 1 saves (no half-saves); a visible-but-empty leg 2 is skipped silently; ✕ removes the section. Editing stays per-card (combined edit deliberately rejected as high-churn). Deliberately NOT done: merging legs into one booking object (would touch sync/export/tombstones/renderer for the same visible result).
- **v291:** **Medicine dose-interval advisory + weight-date (council item 3 — design confirmed via 3 AskUserQuestion calls, all recommendations accepted).** SAFETY-SENSITIVE; advisory only, never a block. Reference intervals (shown to the user, never silently applied): **paracetamol ≥4h gap / max 4 in 24h; ibuprofen ≥6h / max 3 in 24h**; Antibiotic gets no rule. Three layers: (1) **always-visible "Last logged" summary** above the chips for each medicine present in the log — neutral grey normally, **amber** when inside the minimum gap or at the 24h max; shows ago-time, clock time, dose, and dose-count when ≥2 in 24h. (2) The amber state itself. (3) **One OK/Cancel confirm at log time** when the entered dose is inside the gap or over the max — checked against the **entered timestamp** (nearest same-medicine dose by absolute distance), so backfilling an old dose compares to its own neighbours, not to "now". Name matching is substring-based: calpol/paracetamol and nurofen/ibuprofen, so free-typed entries count. **Wording rule (from the council dissent, non-negotiable): every message says "logged"** ("There's a Calpol dose *logged* only 1h 40m from this one…", "Based only on logged doses — check the leaflet") so a message can never read as permission when a dose simply wasn't logged. New top-level helpers: `MED_RULES`, `medRuleFor`, `medLastFor`, `medHistoryFor(rule, refTs)`, `medAgoStr`. Also: **dose calculator now shows the weight's date** — `latestBabyWeight()` replaced by `latestBabyWeightRec()` (returns the growth record); the working line reads "8.4kg (logged 12 Jun) x 15mg/kg ~ …" so a stale weight is self-evident. No new data or sync fields — everything reads the existing medicine log. **Council item 5 also closed** (no code): Cathal verified the Firebase rules (`/hearth` read+write require `auth != null`, all else denied) and **disabled account sign-up** in Firebase Auth — the two existing accounts are now the only ones that can ever exist.
- **v290:** **Two bug fixes (both reported by Cathal from live v288).** (1) **Restore confirmation appeared BEHIND the cloud-backups sheet** — the sheet was z-index 950, `confirmDialog` is 900, so tapping Restore looked like nothing happened until the sheet was closed. Sheet dropped to 890 (above the Settings overlay at 400, below confirmDialog). Lesson: hand-built overlay dialogs must slot into the existing z-order — Settings 400 < sheets ~890 < confirmDialog/login 900 < delete-all 950 < toasts/debug 9999. (2) **Intermittent stuck boot screen on phone** — `verifySession`'s silent re-auth (`getAuthToken` → securetoken fetch) has no timeout; every failure path leniently enters the app, but a fetch that *hangs* (flaky mobile radio on app wake) neither succeeds nor fails, so `onLoginSuccess` never ran. Added a 6s boot watchdog: enter the app with stored credentials no matter what; a guard flag makes the late-arriving real callback a no-op. Behaviour on success/failure is unchanged — only the hang case is new.
- **v289:** **Housekeeping bundle (council item 4).** (1) **DOMPurify 3.0.8 vendored locally** — `purify.min.js` now sits in the repo root (exact npm-published minified build; the jsdelivr CDN is unreachable from build sandboxes anyway) and is pre-cached in the sw.js `ASSETS`; removes the app's only third-party runtime dependency and makes the rich-text sanitiser work offline. (2) **Tombstone purge 7d → 30d** (`purgeTombs`) — closes most of the "device offline >purge window resurrects deleted items" gap. (3) **Pre-v242 recipe stamp migration** — on boot, any recipe without `updated` gets a fixed constant stamp of `1` (deliberately NOT `Date.now()`, which would let a stale copy clobber the partner's newer edit; all devices converge on the same constant, and any real edit/deletion still wins). (4) **Touch targets + dose-note legibility:** list checkboxes keep their 26px look but gain a ~44px tap area via an invisible `::before` halo — a near-miss now **checks off** instead of doing nothing (the v257 escalation, implemented); medicine quick-chips grown from ~28px to ~44px tall; the dosing-working note under the dose field bumped 11px → 13px with darker text (it's the safety-relevant line — it shouldn't be the smallest text on screen).
- **v288:** **Cloud backup restore + import overhaul.** New **"☁️ Cloud backups" button in Settings** (under the Last-cloud-backup line) opens a dialog listing the stored snapshots (shallow-keys GET, newest first, friendly date labels); tap **Restore** → `confirmDialog` → the snapshot is fetched and merged in, then both sync channels push and the current view re-renders. The merge is **additive-only** (missing ids added; existing entries never overwritten/deleted; toast reports counts or "nothing to restore"). Under the hood the file-import handler's body was extracted into a shared **`importBackupData(data)`** used by both paths (mirrors the v287 `buildExportPayload` refactor). **Three real import gaps fixed in the shared function:** (1) **trips and notes were exported but never imported** — now merged by id (trips additive at trip level; per-list + global/global_work notes); customCats also now imported (union by name). (2) **Tombstone-resurrection-kill:** restored/imported items, recipes, trips and bookings are stamped `updated=now` and matching local tombstones cleared — without this, restoring something deleted since the snapshot was silently re-deleted by the newest-wins+tombstone merge on the next sync. (Accepted edge case: a restored copy of an id the partner recently edited wins over that edit via the fresh stamp — rare, additive-only.) (3) **File import never pushed** — imported data used to reach the partner only after the next unrelated action; both paths now push both channels immediately. **Not live-tested against Firebase from the sandbox** (headless smoke test covers boot + function presence + the merge logic locally); live app is the real confirmation.
- **v287:** **Daily automatic cloud backup.** Once a day, ~8s after login (and on resume-from-background >30s), each device quietly PUTs the full export payload to the user's own private Firebase area (`/hearth/users/<user>/backups/<YYYY-MM-DD>`). Date-keyed → a second device the same day is an idempotent overwrite; keeps the newest **7** snapshots (`CLOUD_BACKUP_KEEP`), pruning older via a shallow-keys GET + DELETEs after each successful write. Both accounts back up independently (free redundancy). Silent on any failure — retries next open, logs "cloud backup skipped" to console only. Settings shows **"Last cloud backup: <date time>"** under the existing Last-backup line (`fl4_last_cloudbackup`, transient device-local, NOT exported). `exportAllData` was refactored: payload construction extracted to **`buildExportPayload()`** (top-level), shared by the file export and cloud backup so coverage can never drift — the coverage-checklist comment moved with it. **Manual export nudge relaxed 7 → 30 days** (design decision: the downloaded file now only guards against losing Firebase itself; cloud backup covers everyday risks). **Design confirmed with Cathal up front** (three AskUserQuestion calls): backup writer ships alone with the **restore UI as a follow-up version (v288 next)**; nudge stretched to 30 days (not kept at 7, not removed); 7 dailies retention (weekly tail rejected). **Restore path until v288:** ask Claude Code to pull a snapshot from Firebase, or use the Firebase console. **Not live-tested against Firebase from the build sandbox** — same REST/auth pattern as `pushPersonal` (`userUrl` + `?auth=` token, no-token fallback for legacy keyless setups); live app is the real confirmation.
- **v286:** **Offline app-shell fix (sw.js).** The fetch handler used to *deliberately not intercept* `/` and `/index.html` ("always fetch fresh") — so although both were cached at install, they were never served from cache, and an installed PWA opened with no network showed a browser error page instead of the app. Now the shell (navigation requests + `/` + `/index.html`) is **network-first with cache fallback**: online behaviour is identical to before (fresh fetch every open, so updates still land immediately), but on network failure the cached copy is served (with `ignoreSearch` + `./index.html` as the final fallback). Each successful shell fetch also refreshes the cached copy, so the offline fallback tracks the live version. Other same-origin assets stay cache-first; `sw.js` itself stays never-intercepted. Found by the v285 full-app council review (unanimous #1 priority). Accepted trade-off (flagged by Devil's Advocate): when *offline*, a newly-activated SW can serve a one-version-stale shell — harmless here since versions are backward-compatible over localStorage.
- **v285:** **Trips scroll restoration.** Opening a trip saves `window.scrollY`; pressing ← All trips restores it via `requestAnimationFrame` (mirrors the v269 recipe pattern). Expanding a booking card smoothly scrolls to the top of that card (`scrollIntoView({ block:"start" })`); collapsing does not scroll.
- **v284:** **Trip detail: expand/collapse all.** "Expand all" / "Collapse all" text button sits right-aligned in the view-toggle row. Clicking when any card is collapsed expands all; clicking when all are expanded collapses all. Button is hidden when the trip has no bookings.
- **v283:** **Trip booking cards — 24hr times, ghost card style, Maps link.** All trip times switch to 24hr format (`bookingDateTimeLabel` and inline `timeLineHtml` block). Ghost check-out/end cards now highlight their time in teal+bold to match main cards. Expanded card shows location as a tappable 📍 Google Maps link (opens in browser); link click is guarded with `stopPropagation` so it doesn't collapse the card.
- **v282:** **Trip timeline polish.** Date separators enlarged and teal (13px/800 weight, `#0F8A8A`) so they read as section headers. Departure/start time on each card highlighted in teal bold while the rest of the date/end/location line stays grey. Transfer badge centred.
- **v281:** **Trip import: merge into existing trip.** The import flow is now two-step. Step 1: paste JSON (same as before). Step 2: choose to **Create as new trip** (original behaviour) or pick any existing trip from a list to **add the imported bookings directly to it** (merges via `mergeBookingsIntoTrip`). Useful for adding a single booking (e.g. a car rental JSON) to an existing trip without creating a duplicate.
- **v280:** **Trip JSON export + import (Claude pipeline).** `📤 Export trip (JSON)` button in the trip editor downloads `hearth-trip-name.json`. `📥` icon in the Trips header opens a paste panel with the exact Claude prompt to use ("read these booking emails and output a Hearth trip JSON…"). Format: `{"hearth":"trip-v1","name":...,"start":...,"end":...,"bookings":[{type, title, start, end, location, ref, notes}]}`. New IDs generated on import (no collision risk). Round-trips cleanly.
- **v279:** **Fix timeline ghost cards for same-day flights.** Ghost end/checkout cards were appearing for every booking with an end time, including same-day flight arrivals (e.g. departs 5:10pm arrives 7:55pm = still Jul 3). Added a calendar-date guard so ghost cards only generate when start and end are on **different calendar dates** — the intended use case (hotel check-out on a different day, parking return on a different day).
- **v278:** **Timeline view for trips + date separators.** Trip detail gains a **Compact / Timeline** toggle. In Timeline mode, any booking with an end date/time shows a second ghost card (dashed border, read-only) at its end time, sorted into the correct chronological position — so hotel check-outs and parking returns appear on the right day. Both views now show date separators (e.g. "Thu 3 Jul") between day groups. Compact view is unchanged; switching back is the rollback.
- **v277:** **Connecting flight legs with auto-calculated transfer time.** New "Connecting leg" toggle in the booking editor (all types). When on, a `⇄ Transfer · Xh Ym` badge appears above that booking in the timeline, computed from `prevBooking.end → thisBooking.start`. Hidden if gap ≤ 0 or > 12 hours. Stored as a `connectsFrom` boolean on each booking; rides the existing booking-level merge with no new sync fields.
- **v276:** **Fix booking editor closing on sync.** Opening a trip or booking editor then receiving a sync event caused the editor to close immediately — both `applyPersonal` and `applyHousehold` called `renderTrips()` unconditionally when the Trips section was visible, rebuilding the view and wiping the open form. Fixed by adding `&& !_tripEditing` guards to both sync paths, identical to the recipe-editor fix in v248. The merge still runs and data is stored; only the repaint is held until the editor closes.
- **v275:** **New Trips section — multi-trip itinerary tracker.** 7th top-level nav icon (🧳, hideable via Settings like Train/Notes/Home, placed at the **very end** of the bottom bar — confirmed via direct question). Named **"Trips"**, not "Travel," to avoid colliding with the existing Travel packing list inside Lists. Create multiple trips (name + date range); each trip holds **bookings** — type (🅿️ parking / ✈️ flight / 🚗 car rental / 🏨 accommodation / 🎫 other), title, date/time (start, optional end), location, ref, notes — that **auto-sort into a timeline**; tap a card to expand full details. Trip list splits into **Upcoming / Past** so history doesn't grow unbounded. Each leg of a multi-leg journey (e.g. outbound/return flights) is its own separate booking card. **Sync is booking-level, not trip-level:** new shared helper `mergeTripsData` does a nested two-level newest-wins-by-`updated` merge (trip, then each trip's bookings), with tombstones at both levels (`fl4_tomb_trips`, `fl4_tomb_bookings`) — so two devices editing different bookings in the same trip merge cleanly instead of one clobbering the other. Synced via both `pushPersonal` and `pushHousehold`, same as recipes/lists. Distinct teal accent (`#0F8A8A`). Deferred to a later version: countdown to departure, linking to the Travel packing list, per-traveller info (passport expiry), budget. **Design reviewed and confirmed up front** (naming collision, sync granularity, nav placement, hideability, accent colour all explicitly agreed before building).
- **v274:** **Weather follows device location (for travel).** The weather card/sheet now tries `navigator.geolocation.getCurrentPosition()` on every fetch (8s timeout, 10min `maximumAge`) so it shows local weather automatically while travelling — built ahead of a Germany trip. On denial/timeout/unsupported browser, **silently falls back** to the existing hardcoded Carrigaline coords (the v258 design) — can only improve on prior behaviour, never break it. Card label and sheet stamp show **"Current location"** when using device GPS, **"Carrigaline"** on fallback (new `data.loc` field, cached alongside the rest of the weather record). **Confirmed design up front** (two AskUserQuestion calls): auto-detect-with-fallback over a manual Settings toggle; generic "Current location" label over an extra reverse-geocoding call to show the actual city name. Coords used only to build the Open-Meteo URL — never stored beyond the existing device-local `hearthWeather` cache, never synced, no new third-party service.
- **v273:** **Hourly weather forecast strip.** The weather detail sheet (v262) now shows a horizontally-scrollable **next-24-hours** strip — icon, temp, rain % (only when ≥20%) per hour — sitting between the current-conditions header and the 3-day rows. Open-Meteo request extended with `&hourly=temperature_2m,weather_code,precipitation_probability` (same free no-key endpoint already in use). Rolling 24h window from the current hour (not calendar-day-aligned), so it always covers "the rest of today + into tomorrow." Cached alongside the existing data in `hearthWeather` — still device-local, never synced, no `sw.js` change (cross-origin fetch untouched). Freshness gate now also requires `cached.hours` so an older cache refetches once. Home-card summary and 3-day daily rows unchanged.
- **v272:** **Manual amount editing on Grocery items.** The item edit sheet now shows an **Amount** field for Grocery items (hidden on all other lists). The `item.amount` field (e.g. "200 g", "1 can") was already stored and shown as a chip on grocery cards but could only be set via recipe import — now it can be set or cleared manually from the edit sheet. No change to other lists or sync logic.
- **v271:** **Fix duplicate items from home dashboard quick-add.** `doHqpAdd()` (the home quick-add panel for Grocery and To-do) had no duplicate check — if "Milk" was already in the Grocery list, tapping Add from the home dashboard would silently create a second "Milk". The regular list-section add already guarded against this. Added the same logic: shows "Already in your list!" for undone duplicates; unchecks done items instead of re-adding. Also replaced an incorrect `saveCurrentList()` call (which saved/pushed `currentList` instead of `lt`, so if you'd last been on a different list type the wrong list was pushed) with direct push calls scoped to `lt`; added missing `updated` timestamp to items created from the dashboard.
- **v270:** **Scroll-to-top on all navigation transitions.** Opening a recipe detail (`window.scrollTo(0,0)`), switching list types (Grocery → Travel → To-do etc.), and switching sections (Home / Lists / Recipes / Baby etc.) all scroll to the top of the page so you start at the beginning of the new view. `CLAUDE.md` backup-branch step made mandatory and explicit.
- **v269:** **Recipe search + scroll restoration on back.** A 🔍 Search button appears above the recipe list (static HTML, survives re-renders); toggles a fuzzy-search input; filters by recipe name + notes in real time. When you open a recipe detail, `window.scrollY` is saved; pressing Back re-renders the list and restores your scroll position. Also added `CLAUDE.md` always-create-PR rule.
- **v268:** **Recipe deletion sync via tombstones.** Deleting a recipe now records a tombstone (`fl4_tomb_recipes`, same helpers as the v245 list tombstones: `getTombs`/`addTomb`/`purgeTombs`/`mergeTombs`). Both sync channels (household and personal) carry `rb_deleted` in their payloads; on apply, tombstoned recipes are removed if `deletedAt > recipe.updated` (newest-wins — if a device edited the recipe after you deleted it, the edit survives). A convergence re-push fires if any recipes were pruned. **Bonus fix:** `pushPersonal` was never including `recipebook` — same-account devices (e.g. phone + laptop) were missing recipe sync on the personal channel entirely; fixed.
- **v267:** **Feels-like temperature on weather card.** The home-page weather card now shows "Feels X°" at the top of the right-side meta area (above H/L and Humidity). The `apparent_temperature` value was already being fetched from Open-Meteo and stored as `rec.feels` — it just wasn't displayed. Guarded against null so it only appears when the API returns a value. Also added `CLAUDE.md` and `HEARTH-notes.md` to the repo.
- **v265–v266:** **Version-label timestamp clarified, then simplified.** Diagnosed a "wrong time" report on the bottom-of-page label: it is **not a live clock** — the HTML label is overwritten at runtime by the `sw.js` `VERSION` constant via the `SW_VERSION` postMessage, so it always shows the *active service worker's* build stamp and can lag until the new SW activates. v265 was a no-op timestamp bump to test this; v266 **dropped the `HH:MM` from the format** (now `vNNN · DD/MM/YYYY`) so a frozen build stamp can never be misread as a stale clock. No logic change. Also established: previewing the full PWA in the Claude file-preview pane shows blank because the sandbox blocks the external DOMPurify CDN / inline scripts — the app renders fine deployed (verified via headless boot producing the full DOM); the preview pane is not a reliable way to view this app.
- **v264:** **Weight-based medicine dosing.** Medicine chips now compute a default dose from her **latest logged weight** (`latestBabyWeight()`, new top-level helper — newest growth record with a numeric `weight`). Per-chip config lives in `data-` attrs: **Calpol** = 15mg/kg, 120mg/5ml (Calpol Infant); **Nurofen** = 10mg/kg, 100mg/5ml (Nurofen for Children); Antibiotic = no calc ("dose as prescribed"). Formula `ml = weight × mgkg × 5 / mg5`, **rounded DOWN to 0.25ml** (suggestion never exceeds the mg/kg calc), **capped at 10ml** single dose. **Floor guard:** Nurofen **blocked under 5kg** (ibuprofen contraindicated) — fills no dose, shows a check-GP note. **No weight logged →** falls back to flat 5ml/2.5ml with a "log a weight" note. A `#medDoseNote` line under the dose field always shows the working + product strength + "estimate only, check the leaflet" (set via `textContent`, ASCII only). All editable. **Caveat baked into notes, not just the UI:** the ml is only correct for the two confirmed strengths above — switching to **Calpol 6+ (250mg/5ml)** would make the suggestion ~2× too high; the inline strength label is the guard. Uses latest weight regardless of age, so a stale weight gives a stale dose (the note shows the kg used). **Confirmed with Cathal up front; council declined.**
- **v263:** **Baby medicine quick-chips + sex-save fix.** (1) Added **Calpol / Nurofen / Antibiotic** chips inside `renderMedicine()` (the static chips at ~line 1069 are **dead code** — `renderMedicine()` rebuilds the view from scratch, so chips had to go in the render fn). Tapping a chip fills `medName` + a default dose (**Calpol 5ml, Nurofen 2.5ml**, Antibiotic blank) then focuses/selects the dose field — fill-and-edit, not auto-log. (2) **Fixed `babySex` not persisting:** the household-apply merge (`applyHousehold`, ~line 5269) rebuilt the baby object via `storeSet("fl4_baby", {...})` and **silently omitted `babySex`**, wiping it every sync cycle. Added `babySex: local.babySex || rb.babySex` (local wins, falls back to partner's device). The import path already had a guard; this was the missing second path.
- **v262:** **Weather detail sheet (3-day).** Tapping the weather strip opens a **bottom sheet** (existing `.overlay`/`.modal-box` pattern, × + backdrop-tap to close) showing today in detail (temp, condition, feels-like, humidity) and **three day-rows** (Today/Tomorrow/weekday: icon, high/low, 💧 rain chance, 💨 max wind in km/h), plus the "as of HH:MM" stamp. The existing fetch was **extended from 1 day to 3** (added `apparent_temperature`, `precipitation_probability_max`, `wind_speed_10m_max`) and the whole forecast caches in `hearthWeather`, so the sheet opens instantly from cache and works offline. Opening also triggers a **forced refresh** (`fetchWeather(true)`) so an older cache lacking 3-day data still fills in. Still **device-local, no Firebase, no SW change** (Open-Meteo stays cross-origin). **Not live-tested against the API from the build sandbox** (Open-Meteo blocked there) — field names are Open-Meteo's documented standard; live app is the real confirmation.
- **v261:** **Home layout cleanup.** (1) **★ Today spotlight moved above Grocery.** (2) **"Active items" card removed** — its HTML, its populating JS, *and* its click handler all deleted (a dead `getElementById("tcLifeCard")` would have thrown). (3) **Training card → compact full-width strip** mirroring the Baby card's horizontal layout (icon left, "Training" + status middle, session count + label right); kept its original IDs (`tcTrainCount`/`tcTrainLabel`/`tcTrainSub`) so the session/kcal logic and tap-to-train behaviour are unchanged. Home order now: greeting → weather → Training strip → Baby strip → quick-add → ★ Today → Grocery → week strip.
- **v260:** **Front-page to-do = today's picks.** The home-page to-do spotlight now shows **only tasks starred for today** (the v256 `today` boolean), and the section header is relabelled **"★ Today"**. Empty state reads **"🎉 No tasks for today"**. Implemented by adding a `todayOnly` flag to the shared `renderSpotlight(elId, lt, maxItems, todayOnly)` — grocery passes nothing and is **unchanged**; todo passes `true` and filters to `!x.done && x.today`. Completing a task auto-clears its `today` flag (v256), so it drops off the front page. **"See all →" still opens the full General list** (where the ★ Today filter chip lives). **Trade-off accepted:** on a day nothing is starred, the block reads "No tasks for today" even with a full General list — by design, it surfaces only deliberately-picked tasks.
- **v259:** **Weather timestamp always visible.** The "as of HH:MM" reading time now shows **always**, not only when stale, so the reading's age is visible at a glance. The stale styling (card dims at >30 min) is unchanged. Tiny display tweak.
- **v258:** **Home-page weather card.** A slim card under the greeting shows current temp + condition emoji, with high/low and humidity on the right, for **Carrigaline (hardcoded coords 51.81, -8.39)**. Source is **Open-Meteo** (`api.open-meteo.com`) — **no API key, no signup**, so nothing secret in the public client file. **Device-local, never synced** (not via Firebase / union-by-id). Cached in `localStorage` under **`hearthWeather`**; refetched only if the reading is **>30 min old**. Degrades gracefully: paints cache immediately (with an "as of HH:MM" stamp when stale), skips network when offline, and on fetch failure with no cache the card **stays hidden** rather than breaking home render. **No `sw.js` logic change needed**: the existing fetch handler only intercepts same-origin requests (`url.origin === self.location.origin`), so the cross-origin Open-Meteo call falls through untouched and is never cached by the SW. Design confirmed up front: **hardcoded coords + minimal card with humidity**.
- **v257:** **Accidental-tap fix — all detail lists.** Tapping just right of an item's checkbox used to open the notes sheet (the card click had a catch-all "everything else opens notes" zone), so a near-miss aimed at the checkbox dropped you into the editor. Notes now open **only when the item body text (`.item-body`) is tapped**; the gap between the checkbox and the body is a **neutral buffer** (does nothing), widened with a 5px left margin on the body. Check-off (the circle) and check-off behaviour are unchanged. Applies to all note-bearing lists (Grocery, General, Personal, Travel). **Long-press-to-open-notes was considered first and rejected** as too fiddly on touch (needs scroll-cancel, post-press click-suppression, native context-menu collision, and a haptic for discoverability). Easy future escalation if near-misses persist: also enlarge the checkbox's own tap target so a near-miss *checks off* instead of doing nothing. **Verified on-device.**
- **v256:** **"Today" pick on the General list.** A ☆/★ star on each General-list item marks it for "today" — a manual per-item **`today` boolean** (**option B**: a focus/filter view, *not* a recurring or daily-reset mechanism). Once anything's starred, a **★ Today (N)** filter chip appears at the top of General; tap to show only today's picks, tap again for all. Ticking an item done **auto-clears** its `today` flag. **No new sync machinery** — the flag rides the existing v245 union-by-id + newest-wins merge via each item's `updated` stamp, so it syncs across devices for free. Deliberately **persistent / no auto-reset** (you un-star or complete) to avoid any day-rollover/double-reset sync hazard. Scoped to General (`todo`) only. Stranded-filter guard: if the filter is on but nothing is starred (e.g. you completed the last pick), it auto-disengages so you can't get stuck in an empty filtered view with no chip to toggle off. **Verified on-device.**
- **v255:** **Partner-added-recipe toast.** Gentle "🍴 Petra added a recipe: \<name\>" (or "N recipes") when a new recipe arrives from the partner. New recipes now carry `addedBy`; the toast fires from the **household channel only** (no same-account double-fire across your own devices) and **skips the first-load bulk** (only on an already-populated device). Existing/unstamped recipes don't trigger it. **Verified on all three devices.** During verification, Petra's tablet briefly stopped syncing (received but couldn't send) — root cause was **low device storage evicting her saved login token/household code**, not a code bug; fixed by freeing space + re-login. (See "Sync troubleshooting" below.)
- **v254:** **`noindex` meta tag** added to `<head>` (`<meta name="robots" content="noindex, nofollow">`) to discourage search-engine discovery of the public Pages site. Protection still relies on the login gate; this only trims the find-via-search surface. README removed from the GitHub repo at the same time (app doesn't need it — Pages serves `index.html`/`sw.js`/`manifest.json`/icons).
- **v251–v253:** **Backup-on-open nudge + date/time.** Dismissible green banner suggests a backup when none in 7 days. Fires on app open: cold open (auto-login already calls `onLoginSuccess`) **and** resume-from-background (`visibilitychange`, only if away >30s, so quick switches don't flash it). "Back up now" runs the existing export (downloaded file) via the refactored `exportAllData()` and records `fl4_last_backup`; ✕ snoozes 2 days (`fl4_backup_snooze`). Backup filename now includes time (`hearth-backup-YYYY-MM-DD_HHMM.json`, no colons). Settings shows **"Last backup: <date time>"** under Export, updated on each export (`fmtBackupTime`/`updateLastBackupInfo`). `fl4_last_backup`/`fl4_backup_snooze` are transient (not exported/synced) — so the nudge is per-device; on Android the installed PWA + Chrome share origin storage, so they share the timestamp. (v252 used temporary 10-min/2-min test thresholds; v253 reverted to 7-day/2-day and added the filename time + Settings line.) **Tested: cold open + 10-min reshow on PWA.**
- **v250:** **Recipe method formatting (render-time, non-destructive).** Imported methods rendered as one run-on block because the detail view used `esc(r.method)`, which doesn't render newlines — but `cleanText` preserves newlines on import, so the breaks were in storage all along (a render bug, not a data problem). New `formatMethod()` honours line breaks (single → `<br>`, blank line → paragraph) and splits run-together numbered steps ("…cool. 2. Whisk…") onto their own line (conservative: 1–2 digit + ". ", so "350." / "1.5" are safe). Stored text untouched; the editor `<textarea>` still shows raw text. Applies to all recipes, no migration. **Verified on-device.**
- **v249:** **Bulk recipe categorising.** "✓ Select" button at the top of the Recipes list → selection mode (checkboxes; tap a card to select). Bottom bar "Set category (N)" + Cancel; picker offers chips of existing categories, a new-category field, and "Set to Uncategorised". `applyBulkCategory()` sets the category on all selected, stamps each `updated` (syncs via newest-wins), and toasts the count. Selection works within the current filter (filter to Uncategorised → Select → set = fast way to clear the import backlog). **Verified on-device.** Note: built after a workspace-drift incident (a phantom "v249" with this feature appeared in the build copy but was never in the deployed app) — rebuilt cleanly on the re-uploaded real v248.
- **v248:** **Recipe editor no longer closes mid-edit.** The recipe-sync re-render (`if (currentSection === "recipes" …) renderRecipes()` in `applyHousehold`/`applyPersonal`) used to repaint over the open editor when a sync landed, discarding unsaved input. Added a `_recipeEditing` flag (set in `openRecipeEditor`, cleared on save, cancel, and at the top of the `renderRecipes` dispatcher so it can't stick); both sync re-renders now skip while it's true. Incoming recipe changes are still merged/stored during editing — they just render on close. **Verified on-device.**
- **v247:** **List convergence re-push (the missing half).** With ~simultaneous adds on 3 devices, each pushes its whole list to the single `/shared` doc, so a later push can overwrite an earlier one before every device's SSE saw it — leaving `/shared` missing an item, and a device that only ever received the incomplete version (e.g. the laptop) never got it. The v245 merge kept local items but, for lists, never re-pushed the fuller set. Now `mergeListItems()` returns `{items, push}`; when our merged set is fuller/newer than incoming (or a tombstone needs propagating), both channels schedule a convergence re-push so `/shared` accumulates the union. Self-terminates once all devices agree. (Recipes have had this since v242 via `rbNeedPush`; lists were missing it.) **Confirmed working on three devices** — the 1/2/3 simultaneous-add case now converges without navigating.
- **v246:** **Grace window removed — sync applies immediately.** Incoming SSE events were dropped for a rolling ~5s window after any local push (`SSE_GRACE_MS`, effectively 5000 via the duplicate decl), and every action — firing both a personal- and household-channel push — kept bumping that window, so during active use changes only applied ~5s after activity settled (the "navigate away to make the 2nd action show" symptom; personal channel dropped with no catch-up at all). Now obsolete: the v245 merge (union + newest-wins + tombstones) preserves local unpushed changes and makes a self-echo an idempotent no-op. Both `handleHouseholdEvent` and `handlePersonalEvent` now **apply immediately**. Removed both duplicate `SSE_GRACE_MS` declarations and the `_graceRefetch` timer. (`lastPushTs` still set on push but no longer read — trivial dead assignment.)
- **v245:** **List sync → union-by-id + newest-wins + tombstones.** Replaced the household **wholesale-replace** (which lost concurrent adds — the "grocery items vanish" bug, where `items: incoming` overwrote the local set) with a **union-by-id merge**: per-item `updated` newest-wins + deletion **tombstones** (`fl4_tomb_<lt>`, 7-day purge), applied identically on the household and personal channels. New shared helpers near `saveCurrentList`: `getTombs` / `addTomb` / `purgeTombs` / `mergeTombs` / `mergeListItems`. Items stamped `updated` on add / check-off / category-change; tombstones recorded on removeItem, clear-completed, and recipe-tag delete, and pushed in both list payloads (`deleted:`). Tombstones are transient sync metadata — deliberately **not exported/synced**. (This is the merge the rest of the sync notes refer back to; v247 later added the list convergence re-push that v242 already had for recipes.)
- **v244:** **Category quick-pick chips.** Recipe editor's Category field now shows previously-used categories as tappable chips beneath it (reliable on Android, where the `<datalist>` dropdown often didn't surface). Tap to fill; tap the active chip to clear; highlight stays synced if you type. Free-text field + desktop datalist retained. New helper `recipeCategoryChips()`. Pure UI, no sync/storage impact.
- **v243:** **Sync send-wedge fixed (`try/finally`).** `applyHousehold` ran `renderRecipes()`/`renderBabyView()` inside the `syncPaused = true … = false` window with no exception guard — so if a render/merge threw mid-sync (e.g. while sitting on the Recipes section during an incoming update), `syncPaused` never reset and `pushHousehold` bailed forever after: that device became **receive-only, unable to send**. Now the whole body is wrapped in `try { … } catch { console.warn } finally { syncPaused = false; }`, so a throw can never wedge sending again; errors are logged and sync continues. **Confirmed working on-device** (Cathal's phone + laptop + Petra's tablet all sending/receiving). Diagnostic aid: any future apply error now surfaces as "applyHousehold error (sync continues)" in the console.
- **v242:** **Recipe sync → newest-wins by `updated`.** Replaced v240's merge-by-id / incoming-wins (which clobbered edits whenever a stale device pushed) with **newest-wins by per-recipe `updated` timestamp** plus a convergence re-push (`rbNeedPush`) when the local book is fuller/newer — applied on **both** the household and personal channels (the personal channel previously never merged the recipebook at all, so same-account devices could diverge). Every recipe is now stamped `updated` on create/edit/fav/import. Accepted costs (carried forward): recipe **deletions don't propagate**, and concurrent edits to the same recipe resolve newest-edit-wins (older edit lost).
- **v241:** **Sync grace-period catch-up fix.** `handleHouseholdEvent` used to silently drop any SSE event arriving within the post-push grace window (`SSE_GRACE_MS`, effectively 5s) — so a remote change landing just after a local push was lost until the next event/focus. Now it schedules a single catch-up `fetchHousehold()` once the window closes (`_graceRefetch`), so remote edits reconcile instead of being missed. A pull never pushes, so no bounce; self-echo becomes an idempotent no-op. **Note:** the duplicate `SSE_GRACE_MS` declaration (3000 at line ~1795, 5000 at ~4357 — second wins) was left in place; the identical silent-drop bug in `handlePersonalEvent` (personal channel) was also left for a follow-up.
- **v240:** **Recipes shared household-wide (merge-by-id).** Recipe book now syncs through the existing household `/shared` channel. Pushed on every mutation (all route through `saveRecipeBook`, which now calls `pushHousehold()`); `data["recipebook"]` added to the `pushHousehold` payload (always shared when a household is set, not gated by a per-section toggle). On pull, `applyHousehold` **merges by id**: partner's new recipes added to the top, same-id recipes take the incoming version. New-recipe ids hardened with a random component to avoid cross-device collision.
- **v239:** **Recipe section headers fixed.** Lines like `<b>Base</b>` / `<b>Veg</b>` (or a line ending in ":") were being parsed as ingredients and pushed to the grocery list. Now detected via `isHeaderIng()` / `headerLabel()`: rendered as small uppercase **sub-headings** in the recipe detail view, and **excluded from the grocery push and the saved chip**. Detection is pattern-based, so it rescues already-saved recipes with no re-import. `parseIngredients` also flags new header lines with `header:true`.
- **v238:** **Add to grocery — Phase 2 (amounts).** Grocery items now carry a separate **`item.amount`** field (Path B — amount is NOT baked into the name, so the name stays the clean dedupe key and no freeform-text parsing is needed). `cardHTML` renders it as a muted chip after the name (e.g. "Broccoli 200 g"); shows only when the field is present, so all other items / lists are untouched. Amounts scale at the **current stepper servings** (not base) via the existing `scaleIngredient`; Unicode fractions converted to ASCII ("½"→"1/2"); falls back to name-only when there's no clean amount (incl. the compound-amount parser gap). **New items get amounts; existing items are re-tagged only (their text/amount is never touched).** Saved chip in `fl4_recipes` still names-only at base servings. `addRecipeToGroceries` now takes `(recipe, mult)`.
- **v237:** **Add to grocery list from a recipe (Phase 1 — names only).** New 🛒 button on the recipe detail view. Pushes each ingredient *name* onto the Grocery list tagged with the recipe name (reuses the existing recipe-tag clear/revert machinery — existing items re-tagged with prior state recorded, not deleted). Also upserts a reusable saved-recipe chip in `fl4_recipes` at the recipe's **base servings** (quiet overwrite if the name already exists). New top-level fn `addRecipeToGroceries(recipe)` targets grocery directly (independent of `currentList`), with its own grocery-keyword category detection.
- **v236:** **Recipe categories + favourites.** Star toggle on cards and detail view; "★ Favourites" filter chip; Category field in editor (with suggestions); filter bar per category; "All" view groups recipes by category with an "Uncategorised" group. Imported recipes start uncategorised.
- **v235:** Fixed a null `addEventListener` crash — recipe import sheet HTML sits after the main script, so its wiring is now deferred to DOM-ready and null-guarded.
- **v234:** **Batch CSV recipe import** — RFC-4180 CSV parser handles quoted fields with embedded newlines/commas; maps title→name, quantity→servings, ingredients (parens stripped), instructions→method, source→notes; skips ingredient-less article rows; skips name-duplicates. Upgraded ingredient parser (fractions "1 1/2"/"1/3"/unicode, unit synonyms, count units that pluralise). Tested against the real 88-row MyRecipeBox export: 85 imported, 3 skipped.
- **v233:** Unit rule adjusted — tsp/tbsp **stay** tsp/tbsp at any serving; cups convert to grams (dry, via density table) or ml (liquid).
- **v232:** Built the **Recipes section** (6th nav icon). Recipe objects: name, servings, ingredients (amount/unit/name), method, notes. Servings stepper rescales live. Seeded with the Iced Coffee recipe. Create/edit via a raw-text editor.
- **v231:** Fixed Notes sub-tab flashing on Grocery at login (set it hidden by default to match the switch-handler logic; Grocery has no Notes sub-tab by design — notes are per-item).
- **v230:** Silenced routine "SSE stale — falling back to fetch" messages from the red error panel (changed `console.warn` → `console.log`; genuine SSE event-error warnings kept).

## Completed backlog items (moved out of `HEARTH-backlog.md` § Pending)

Finished backlog entries, kept for their reasoning and "do NOT" warnings. Moved here verbatim so the backlog's Pending list only holds open work. First move 22/09/2026; later moves go at the TOP of this list. "The changelog entry above" inside these entries means the version's entry earlier in this file.

- ~~**Raw record ids in `data-*` attributes — follow-up from the v483 security review.**~~ — **shipped v484** (25/09/2026). About 100 render sites wrote a record's `id` straight into a single-quoted `data-*` attribute with no `esc()`; every one now wraps the value at render, `dataset` decoding it back unchanged. Five `querySelector` id-lookup sites and a handful of pre-escaped chip-renderer locals were deliberately left alone — see the v484 changelog entry above for why. Test: `79-record-id-attribute-escaping.js`.
- ~~**Found while building v424, NOT fixed: `applyHousehold`'s `flushSyncRenders` call never passes `watchlist`.**~~ — **shipped v445** (30/08/2026, alongside the audit's F1/F5; `watchlist:_rDirty.watchlist` added to BOTH calls). Original entry: `applyHousehold` sets `_rDirty.watchlist = true` when a partner's watchlist edit merges in, but the flush call at the end of the function lists `recipes/trips/baby/appliances/list/dots/home` only — so a watchlist change arriving over the HOUSEHOLD channel doesn't redraw the open Watchlist section (the personal channel's call has the same omission). Harmless-ish: the next entry into the section re-renders from storage, so nothing is lost, it just looks stale until you leave and come back. Fix is adding `watchlist:_rDirty.watchlist` to both calls, and it wants the same one-line treatment for any future section.
- ~~**Recipe Focus mode ("highlight only the next line")**~~ — **shipped v421** (12/08/2026: `🔦 Focus` toggle pill on the recipe detail hint row, `_recipeFocusKey` cursor walking ingredients → prep → method in DOM order, `.rcp-focusing`/`.rcp-now` CSS, `fl4_cookfocus` device-local flag — see the v421 changelog entry above for the full shape and the two design-chat calls flagged for Cathal's review on the PR: jump-back clears the tick, no wrap at the end). **Revised v423 (17/08/2026): a tap in Focus no longer crosses the line out at all** — Cathal's call after using it; greying/striking a line is classic (Focus off) behaviour only, so the jump-back-clears-the-tick call is moot and gone. No wrap still stands. Nothing left open.
  - **Tests:** extend `tests/cases/36-recipe-cook-ticks.js` or add a sibling case — pin that focus off is byte-for-byte today's behaviour, that the cursor starts at the first ingredient, both tap rules, the remembered flag surviving a re-entry, and that **nothing is written to the stored recipe** (the `updated === 1` assertion pattern already in 36).
- ~~**Bug queue, 03/08/2026 (Cathal: "add recipes to global search and let's tackle all the recently found bugs one by one")**~~ — **CLOSED, all six shipped:** ① recipe null-crash → v385, ② recipe-search state bugs → v386, ③ recipes-in-global-search → v387, ④ normaliseNotes null guard → v388, ⑤ sw.js cache staleness → v389, ⑥ applyPersonal syncPaused parity → v390. Nothing outstanding from this queue.
- ~~Global search~~ — **shipped v384** (03/08/2026: 🔍 row on Home, `globalSearch()` over Lists+Notes, `openRecord()` deep-link helper, per `HEARTH-global-search.md`); **Recipes phase 2 shipped v387** (03/08/2026, see that changelog entry). Still open per the design doc's §9: **item-level deep links** (needs new design). ~~**`normaliseNotes()` has no null guard**~~ — **shipped v388** (03/08/2026: fixed at the source in `normaliseNotes()` itself, not just worked around locally in `globalSearchNotesPool()` — see the v388 changelog entry above).
- ~~**Run the tidy pass at CSV import time (the v366 council's recorded dissent, 29/07/2026)**~~ — **shipped v367** (30/07/2026): `recipesFromCSV` now runs every parsed ingredient through `tidyIngredient()`, keeping only confident rewrites; the import preview shows "N recipes found · M ingredient lines tidied". See the v367 changelog entry.
- ~~**Case-only merge groups are busywork (v366 council item ④)**~~ — **built in v366's third pass** (29/07/2026, Cathal asked for it directly): the `tidyPickCommon` button resolves every non-synonym group to its most common spelling in one tap, and deliberately leaves the synonym groups alone. Nothing preselected, so "show duplicates, I decide" still holds.
- ~~Re-adding a recipe at different servings doesn't refresh the Amount field it wrote itself~~ — **shipped v370** (31/07/2026, full detail in the changelog entry above). Fix was exactly as scoped: the `_recipePrev` snapshot is now also taken in the NEW-item branch (`amount: ""`), so a later re-add correctly recognises the field as recipe-owned. Nothing left open on this item.
- ~~**Recipe-parser second pass**~~ — **shipped v365** (28/07/2026, full detail in the changelog entry above). Design chat done same day on Fable via PR #131, all 4 decisions built exactly as confirmed — scope = compound amounts + ranges only (alternatives dropped, multi-unit brackets skipped), existing data handled via rescue-at-render (not migration), grocery amount chip shows the scaled range string. Nothing left open on this item.
- ~~**Meal-plan rotation + weekly roll-over**~~ — **shipped v362** (27/07/2026, full detail in the changelog entry above). Both design calls built exactly as confirmed 27/07/2026 — rolling day-key window kept (no ISO-week re-key), roll-over is prompt-first (banner, nothing mutates shared data without a tap). `inRotation`/`lastCooked` recipebook fields, the Fill algorithm, bulk-onboarding via select mode, the ✓-cooked control, and the weekly banner are all built; nothing left open on this item.
- ~~**Duplicate workout-session functions — investigate which copy is live**~~ — **shipped v351** (25/07/2026, full detail in the changelog entry above). Verdict on the open question: inert dead code — all 24 pairs byte-identical, dead copies deleted surgically (7 interleaved live helpers kept), plus the legacy sync-state duplicate declarations (`fbUrl`/`syncRoom`/`syncPaused`/`lastPushTs`/`syncPrefs`); CI `ALLOWLISTED_DUPES` emptied so any future duplicate fails the build.
