- ~~Scout finds during the back-button sweep (16/07/2026)~~ — **resolved, shipped v335**: both (a) the dead `#noteEditorOverlay`/`openListNoteEditor`/`closeNoteEditor` and (b) the `renderNotesIn`→`openNoteEditor` signature mismatch turned out to be the same orphaned pre-v318 per-list notes subsystem, confirmed fully unreachable from the UI and removed wholesale (full detail in the v335 changelog entry above).
- ~~Family Activity Log~~ — **shipped v338** (backlogged 06/07/2026; design confirmed 18/07/2026 and **built the same day on Cathal's go-ahead** — full build detail in the changelog entry above; triage notes + confirmed spec kept below as the decision record). Not in v1, each needing its own design confirm before adding: Home/Today-card teaser, search, teeth (no dates stored — would need new logging first). A read-only, chronologically day-grouped "family story" view aggregating significant events from data the app already timestamps: Baby growth/medicine/milestones/teeth, completed Train sessions, trip starts/ends, created notes (`createdAt` exists since the notes rework). Zero new logging, zero new data model, no sync changes. Agreed guardrails from the triage: **hideable section, default hidden** (v308 Sports precedent — no 8th permanent nav icon); significant events only (grocery adds would spam it); ~90-day default window + "load earlier"; medicine rows factual-only ("Calpol 5 ml · 18:40 · check the leaflet") deep-linking into Baby — **all dosing/interval logic stays exclusively in the Baby flow, never duplicated here**; rows deep-link to their source section via existing `switchSection`/modal patterns. v1 scope call from the triage: plain feed + filter chips, **no search** (leaner first cut than the proposal's wireframe). All four open design calls (event list, entry point, filters, layout) were settled with Cathal on 18/07/2026 — see the build spec sub-bullet below. One data-model finding from that chat: **teeth have no dates at all** (`bd.teeth` is a bare `{toothId:true}` map), so teeth are excluded outright rather than deferred — including them would need new logging, breaking the zero-new-data-model guardrail. Reflective nice-to-have, not daily utility — does NOT jump the queue over Cathal-requested items (per-person packing checks, nav consolidation; cold-start flash shipped as v312). Baby-related wording goes through council review per the safety rules.
  - **Confirmed v1 build spec (design chat 18/07/2026; shipped as v338 the same day — kept as the decision record):**
    - **Entry point: own hideable section, default hidden** — replicate the v308 Sports wiring exactly (6 touch points): add `"famlog"` to `HIDEABLE_SECTIONS`, an entry in the `navIds` map, a `bnFamlog` nav button (`display:none` by default, like `bnSports`), a section `<div>`, a `switchSection` branch that calls the new `renderFamlog()` on entry, and a Settings `.section-chk` toggle. Nav label **"Family Log"**, icon 📖. **No Home/Today-card teaser in v1** (explicitly deferred; revisit only if the section proves used).
    - **Event sources (final list) — pure render-time aggregation from existing stores; zero new data model, zero new logging, zero sync changes:**
      - Baby medicine (`fl4_baby` → `.medicine`, fields `{name, dose, ts}`): row like "💊 Calpol · 5 ml · 18:40 · check the leaflet". **Factual only — all dosing/interval logic stays exclusively in the Baby flow, never duplicated here; never drop the "check the leaflet" wording.**
      - Baby growth (`.growth`, `{date, weight, height}`): "📏 Growth: 9.2 kg · 74 cm".
      - Baby milestones (`.milestones`, `{text, date}`): "⭐ <text>".
      - Train completed sessions (`fl4_workouts` → `.workouts`, `{date, sessionName, kcal}`): "💪 <sessionName> · N kcal".
      - Trip boundaries (`fl4_trips`, `{name, start, end}`): "🧳 Trip started: <name>" on `start`, "🏠 Trip ended: <name>" on `end`; skip a boundary whose date is missing.
      - Notes created (`fl4_notes_global` + `fl4_notes_global_work`, `{title, createdAt}`): "📝 Note: <title>" (`createdAt` is reliable — the editor backfills it from `id` on legacy notes).
      - **Excluded, decided — do not re-add without a new design chat:** teeth (no dates, see above); cardio entries (Cathal's call, 18/07); adult bodyweight/BP (personal health telemetry, not family-story material); sports (API fixture cache, not household activity); grocery/list adds (spam).
    - **Layout:** one flat feed, newest first, grouped under date separators (borrow the Trips timeline date-separator pattern); each row = icon + one-line text + time, where the time is shown only when a real timestamp exists (medicine `ts`, note `createdAt`) — date-only entries (growth, milestones, train, trips) show no time. Whole row taps through to its source section via the existing `switchSection` patterns (`baby` / `train` / `trips` / notes → `lists`).
    - **Filters:** chip bar **All / Baby / Train / Trips / Notes** above the feed (same pattern as the Recipes filter bar). **No search in v1.**
    - **Window:** last 90 days by default; a "Load earlier" button at the bottom extends the window by a further 90 days per tap.
    - **Safety:** Baby-related row wording goes through council review before ship, per the safety rules — **not yet run at v338**: the shipped rows use only the pre-agreed factual medicine format; council pass offered in the PR, Cathal's call.
  - **Triage of the other two proposals in the same document (06/07/2026 — do NOT re-chase):** "Hearth Intelligence" daily briefing → **rejected as a feature**: it's the v302 Today card + v306 suggestions + v291 interval advisory re-described; the genuinely new lines are either nags (v302 deliberately chose positive-only), need a nonexistent passport data model, or are surveillance-flavoured pattern-mining ("wife adds yogurt on Fridays"). If a single line ever proves wanted (e.g. "workout due, last done Nd ago") it's a small Today-card addition, not a system. "Hearth Lens" on-device AI vision/OCR → the document itself rejects it and the reasoning is sound (breaks single-file/no-dependency/mid-range-phone constraints); its suggested alternatives are already shipped or backlogged. No action on either.
- ~~Baby growth centile chart — replace the v310 %-of-median chart with true WHO weight-for-length centiles~~ — **shipped v347** (21/07/2026, PR #106, full detail in the changelog entry above). One deviation from the original spec worth recording: `who.int`/`cdc.gov` were both blocked by that session's network policy, so the LMS tables came from a GitHub mirror of the same CDC-redistributed WHO CSVs rather than the primary source directly — the hard "STOP and investigate on any disagreement" rule fired for real (old table vs new data disagreed by up to ~3.5%) and Cathal confirmed proceeding before the build continued. Manual on-device check of the chart + an HSE-book spot-check of a value or two are still outstanding, flagged in the PR.
- ~~Deferred sync hardening — growth & milestones per-entry merge~~ — **shipped v371** (31/07/2026, full detail in the changelog entry above). Design confirmed on Fable 19/07/2026 built exactly as scoped — growth (date-keyed, `updated`+tombstone, the date-change hazard tombstones the old date), milestones (id-keyed, same `mergeMedicine`-style pattern), `mergeBabyData` generalised into shared `mergeIdKeyed`/`mergeDateKeyed` helpers, channel payloads mirror `med_deleted` exactly (`growth_deleted`/`ms_deleted`), backup restore filters both through local tombstones. Nothing left open on this item.
- ~~**Nav consolidation Option B′ — stages 2–4**~~ — **CLOSED 11/08/2026, Cathal's call: the gate question was put to him after three weeks of living with Track and his answer was "the layout's fine".** That is exactly the condition the Devil's Advocate's recorded dissent named — *"stages 2–4 fix nothing Cathal has named — the backlog item should be CLOSED if no named pain emerges after living with Track"* — so **stage 1 (Track, v339) is the whole of Option B′ that ships.** Do NOT build stage 2 (Travel/Packing), stage 3 (Food/Grocery) or stage 4 (Tasks rename) without a fresh request driven by a real named pain; in particular do not re-offer them as tidy-ups. The Petra heads-up that stage 3 required is moot. **Design record kept below in case it is ever revived** — the conditions (section ids never change, config-table hosting, zero data-store moves, distinct icons) remain binding on anything that does revive it. Original entry follows.
- **[SUPERSEDED — see the CLOSED note above] Nav consolidation Option B′ — stage 1 ("Track") shipped v339 (19/07/2026); stages 2–4 designed but GATED on a fresh per-stage go-ahead from Cathal.** History: original Option B (Food = Grocery+Recipes, Tasks = General+Personal+Notes, Travel = Trips+Packing) was deferred 09/07/2026 pending a design chat + council. Revisited 19/07/2026 on Fable: Cathal's actual pains (general log + food log buried under Lists→Notes; weight/BP buried under Train) were NOT what original B fixed, so the design became **B′ = the three B merges + a new "Track" section for the three personal logs** (confirmed via 3 AskUserQuestion answers: one section · named "Track" · built first). Council pass same day, verdict **build-with-changes**, conditions all adopted and binding for the remaining stages: **internal section ids NEVER change** (labels/icons/hosting only — `lists`/`recipes`/`trips` stay; `track` was the sole new id), sub-tab hosting extends the `TRACK_VIEWS`-style config table rather than copying patterns, zero data-store moves in every stage, distinct icons (Track 📈 vs Train 💪), **Petra gets a heads-up before the stage that moves Grocery**. **Recorded dissent (Devil's Advocate): stages 2–4 fix nothing Cathal has named — do not build them by default; the backlog item should be CLOSED if no named pain emerges after living with Track.** Remaining stages, one version/PR each, each needing Cathal's explicit go-ahead first:
  - **Stage 2 — Travel (smallest):** keep section id `trips`, relabel section/title "Travel"; host the packing list as an in-section sub-tab named **"Packing"** (it's the Lists sub-tab `data-lt="travel"`, store `fl4_travel` — store and `currentList` key unchanged, container re-hosted the same way Track re-hosted Log/Food/Body); re-point `tripPackingBtn` (currently jumps `switchSection("lists")` + programmatic travel-tab click) to the local tab; v326 per-person checks ride along untouched; remove the Travel tab from the Lists `typeBar`.
  - **Stage 3 — Food (biggest family-facing change — Petra heads-up first):** keep section id `recipes`, relabel "Food"; sub-tab row Grocery | Recipes | Week plan, with the Grocery tab re-hosting the shared list container locked to `currentList="grocery"`; re-point every grocery deep-link that does `switchSection("lists")`+grocery (Today-card suggestion chips etc. — sweep with the scout before building); remove Grocery from the Lists `typeBar`.
  - **Stage 4 — Tasks (rename only):** keep section id `lists`, relabel nav button/title "Tasks"; remaining sub-tabs General | Personal | Notes.
  - **Gate question for stage 2 (ask after ~a week of Track use):** "Now that the logs have their own Track section, do you still want Packing moved into Trips and Grocery in with Recipes — or is the current layout fine?" A "fine as is" answer closes this item per the recorded dissent.
- ~~Add-to-grocery Phase 3 — real-world purchase quantities~~ — **CLOSED 19/07/2026, Cathal's call via AskUserQuestion (Fable design session): the Devil's-Advocate dissent was heeded.** The coverage cliff (a ~15-staple table converts milk convincingly and visibly fails for everything else) plus permanent table upkeep doesn't clear the bar, and the Phase-2 cooking amounts already on grocery items cover the real need. Do NOT re-chase without a fresh Cathal request driven by an actual shopping pain. Decision record in case it is ever revived (both also confirmed 19/07/2026): display would be show-both ("1200ml (≈ 2× 1L)", advisory only, never overwriting the stored amount) with a ~15–20 Irish-staples static in-code table.
